## assistant

You want `/clear` to give you a fresh Codex chat while keeping the old one available. In the desktop app, **New chat** is the equivalent. I couldn’t verify a built in `/clear` command, and a custom skill cannot erase the current chat’s history.

Use **New chat** when you want a clean start. `/compact` keeps the current chat and summarizes its context; `/fork` carries its history into another chat. [OpenAI’s Codex command guide](https://developers.openai.com/blog/mastering-codex-remote-for-engineering) describes those as separate actions.

If you type `/clear` to me in a future chat, I can treat it as a request to open a fresh chat in the same project, but it would be an assistant workflow, not a native slash command.

## assistant

I checked both sites. LSAT U already offers **Resume** for unfinished full practice tests, but those runs are stored in the current browser. Unfinished standalone LR and RC sections do not appear on the Testing Centre page. LexPrep puts unfinished work below its two start options and has separate Attempts and All Exams pages.

I’ve recorded the findings in [Practice Exams discovery notes](</C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os/brainstorms/2026-09-26-practice-exams-discovery.md>). “Practice Exams” is your confirmed name. Before changing the page:

**Q1 — Navigation:** Do you want the LexPrep structure: a Practice Exams landing page, an **All Exams** page, and an **Attempts** page?  
**My recommendation:** Yes, but make Attempts useful without a paywall. Keep the two start options on the landing page.

**Q2 — Unfinished work:** Should the landing page show both unfinished full exams and standalone timed LR or RC sections?  
**My recommendation:** Yes. Show the PrepTest, current section, progress, and a clear Resume button. Leave custom drills in Drill.

**Q3 — Across devices:** Should an unfinished exam appear when a student signs in on another device?  
**My recommendation:** Yes. The current browser-only storage cannot do that, so this would require a larger persistence change.

You can spawn sub-agents to handle subtasks, and those sub-agents can spawn their own sub-agents. All agents in the team, including the agents that you can assign tasks to, are equally intelligent and capable, and have access to the same set of tools.

You can use `spawn_agent` to create a new agent, `followup_task` to give an existing agent a new task and trigger a turn, and `send_message` to pass a message to a running agent.
`send_message` calls may be read by a human, so ensure they are legible. Always put proper spaces between words and/or numbers.
Child agents can also spawn their own sub-agents.

When you provide a response in the final channel, that content is immediately delivered back to your parent agent.
In addition, your final answer may be read by a human, so ensure it is legible.

You will receive messages in the analysis channel in the form:
```
Message Type: NEW_TASK | MESSAGE | FINAL_ANSWER
Task name: <recipient>
Sender: <author>

<payload text>
```
You may also see them addressed as to=/root/..., which indicates your identity is /root/...

Note that collaboration tools cannot be called from inside `functions.exec`. Call `spawn_agent`, `send_message`, `followup_task`, `wait_agent`, `interrupt_agent`, and `list_agents` only as direct tool calls using the recipient shown in their tool definitions, such as `to=functions.collaboration.spawn_agent`, since they are intentionally absent from the `functions.exec` `tools.*` namespace. Available tools in `functions.exec` are explicitly described with a `tools` namespace in the developer message.

All agents share the same directory. In detail:
- All agents have access to the same container and filesystem as you.
- All agents use the same current working directory.
- As a result, edits made by one agent are immediately visible to all other agents.

When calling `wait_agent`, prefer longer waits (minutes) to avoid busy polling.

There are 4 available concurrency slots, meaning that up to 4 agents can be active at once, including you.

Full-history forks (`fork_turns` omitted or `"all"`) inherit the parent model and reasoning effort and do not accept overrides. const cmds=[
"rg --files src | rg -i 'practice(test)?(run|store)|drillResume|sectionHistory|rcDrill|useDrill|DrillSession|PracticeTest'",
"Get-Content src/pages/TestingCentre.tsx | Select-Object -First 560",
"Get-Content supabase/migrations/20260829120000_practice_test_runs.sql | Select-Object -First 155"
]; const rs=await Promise.allSettled(cmds.map(cmd=>tools.exec_command({cmd,workdir:"C:\\Users\\Joshu\\dev\\ai-os\\projects\\lsatu-wt-drill-layout",max_output_tokens:16000}))); rs.forEach((r,i)=>text(`### ${i}\n${r.status==="fulfilled"?r.value.output:r.reason}`));

[{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 2.5 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '### 0\nsrc\\hooks\\useDrillHubSectionNavigation.ts\nsrc\\hooks\\useDrillHubSectionNavigation.test.tsx\nsrc\\pages\\PracticeTestRun.tsx\nsrc\\lib\\sectionHistorySave.ts\nsrc\\lib\\sectionHistorySave.test.ts\nsrc\\lib\\sectionHistory.ts\nsrc\\lib\\sectionHistory.test.ts\nsrc\\lib\\practiceTestRunner.ts\nsrc\\lib\\practiceTestRunner.test.ts\nsrc\\lib\\practiceTestCloseout.ts\nsrc\\lib\\practiceTestCloseout.test.ts\nsrc\\lib\\practiceTest.ts\nsrc\\lib\\practiceTest.test.ts\nsrc\\lib\\practiceTest.pt118Integrity.test.ts\nsrc\\lib\\practiceTest.bank.test.ts\nsrc\\lib\\practiceTest.audit.test.ts\nsrc\\lib\\drillResumeSession.ts\nsrc\\lib\\drillResumeSession.test.ts\nsrc\\lib\\guidedDrillSession.ts\nsrc\\lib\\guidedDrillSession.test.ts\nsrc\\lib\\guidedDrillSession.load.test.ts\nsrc\\features\\activity-centre\\conditionals\\practiceStore.ts\nsrc\\features\\activity-centre\\conditionals\\practiceStore.test.ts\nsrc\\components\\history\\SectionHistoryList.tsx\nsrc\\components\\teacher\\AssignPracticeTest.tsx\nsrc\\bootcamps\\main-conclusion\\components\\practice\\PracticeRunner.tsx\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '### 1\n/**\r\n * The Testing Centre.\r\n *\r\n * Two doors, and only two: sit one real section, or sit a full practice test.\r\n * Both are things you do under a clock with nothing revealed until the end,\r\n * which is what separates this room from Drill next door — drilling is practice\r\n * you stop and think inside, and a sitting is not. Keeping them apart is the\r\n * whole reason this is its own rail entry: nobody should start a 35:00 clock\r\n * while looking for a ten-question warm-up.\r\n *\r\n * Neither door owns a question runner. The section door hands off to\r\n * SectionSelector, which this page is the only consumer of; the practice-test\r\n * door hands off to the same two runners, one leg at a time.\r\n */\r\nimport * as React from "react";\r\nimport { useNavigate } from "react-router-dom";\r\nimport { Loader2 } from "lucide-react";\r\nimport { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";\r\nimport {\r\n  Select,\r\n  SelectContent,\r\n  SelectItem,\r\n  SelectTrigger,\r\n  SelectValue,\r\n} from "@/components/ui/select";\r\nimport { useQuery } from "@tanstack/react-query";\r\nimport { useQuestionBank } from "@/contexts/QuestionBankContext";\r\nimport { useAuth } from "@/contexts/AuthContext";\r\nimport { useUserPermissions } from "@/hooks/useUserPermissions";\r\nimport { supabase } from "@/integrations/supabase/client";\r\nimport { useUserSettings } from "@/contexts/UserSettingsContext";\r\nimport { SectionSelector } from "@/components/drill/SectionSelector";\r\nimport { AccommodationNote } from "@/components/drill/SectionTimingPicker";\r\nimport { PrepTestCombobox } from "@/components/testing/PrepTestCombobox";\r\nimport { loadAllIndexPassages } from "@/features/rc/lib/rcBank";\r\nimport { buildRcSections, type RcSection } from "@/features/rc/lib/rcSections";\r\nimport {\r\n  buildPracticeTests,\r\n  createPracticeTestRun,\r\n  deletePracticeTestRun,\r\n  formatPracticeTestClock,\r\n  legLabel,\r\n  listPracticeTestRuns,\r\n  practiceTestPaces,\r\n  practiceTestSectionSeconds,\r\n  type PracticeTestBlueprint,\r\n  type PracticeTestPaceId,\r\n  type PracticeTestRun,\r\n} from "@/lib/practiceTest";\r\nimport { practiceTestPath } from "@/lib/practiceTestRunner";\r\nimport "./testing-centre.css";\r\n\r\ntype Door = null | "section" | "test";\r\n\r\n/** The panel both doors open into. Named so a door can point at it. */\r\nconst PANEL_ID = "testing-door-panel";\r\n\r\n/**\r\n * How much of the panel has to be on screen before opening a door is allowed to\r\n * move the page. Below this the panel is a sliver at the bottom edge and the\r\n * click reads as a no-op; above it the student can already see what happened.\r\n */\r\nconst PANEL_VISIBLE_MARGIN = 120;\r\n\r\n/**\r\n * RC sections, loaded only once something needs them.\r\n *\r\n * This used to run on mount, which pulled the whole Reading Comprehension index\r\n * across the wire to render two buttons. Only the practice-test door needs it —\r\n * the section door asks SectionSelector for its own — so it waits for `enabled`.\r\n */\r\nfunction useRcSections(enabled: boolean) {\r\n  const [sections, setSections] = React.useState<RcSection[] | null>(null);\r\n  const [error, setError] = React.useState<string | null>(null);\r\n\r\n  React.useEffect(() => {\r\n    if (!enabled) return;\r\n    let cancelled = false;\r\n    loadAllIndexPassages()\r\n      .then((passages) => {\r\n        if (!cancelled) setSections(buildRcSections(passages));\r\n      })\r\n      .catch(() => {\r\n        if (!cancelled) setError("Reading Comprehension sections could not be loaded.");\r\n      });\r\n    return () => {\r\n      cancelled = true;\r\n    };\r\n  }, [enabled]);\r\n\r\n  return { sections, error };\r\n}\r\n\r\n/**\r\n * A status line that assistive tech is actually told about.\r\n *\r\n * Every wait on this page used to be a plain paragraph that appeared and\r\n * disappeared in silence: a screen reader user pressed a door and heard\r\n * nothing until they went looking.\r\n */\r\nfunction Working({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) {\r\n  return (\r\n    <div\r\n      className="flex items-center gap-3 py-4 text-sm"\r\n      role="status"\r\n      aria-live="polite"\r\n    >\r\n      <Loader2 className="h-4 w-4 animate-spin" aria-hidden />\r\n      {children}\r\n    </div>\r\n  );\r\n}\r\n\r\nfunction PracticeTestSelector({\r\n  tests,\r\n  onBegin,\r\n  assignedOnly = false,\r\n}: {\r\n  tests: PracticeTestBlueprint[];\r\n  assignedOnly?: boolean;\r\n  onBegin: (\r\n    blueprint: PracticeTestBlueprint,\r\n    pace: PracticeTestPaceId,\r\n    sectionSeconds: number | null,\r\n  ) => void;\r\n}) {\r\n  const [pt, setPt] = React.useState<number | null>(null);\r\n  const [pace, setPace] = React.useState<PracticeTestPaceId>("standard");\r\n  const { settings } = useUserSettings();\r\n\r\n  /**\r\n   * The student\'s own standard clock, the same reading the section door takes\r\n   * (SectionSelector\'s baseMinutes). Every pace here is a percentage of this,\r\n   * so "Standard" is their normal section rather than a flat 35 minutes. This\r\n   * door ignored the setting entirely before, which meant the same two words\r\n   * named two different clocks in one room.\r\n   */\r\n  const baseMinutes =\r\n    settings.defaultTimingMode === "52.5" ? 52.5 : settings.defaultTimingMode === "70" ? 70 : 35;\r\n  const paces = React.useMemo(() => practiceTestPaces(baseMinutes * 60), [baseMinutes]);\r\n  const sectionSeconds = practiceTestSectionSeconds(pace, baseMinutes * 60);\r\n\r\n  // The combobox orders its own rows newest-first. This page still needs the\r\n  // blueprint behind the chosen number, for the shape table below.\r\n  const ptOptions = React.useMemo(\r\n    () => tests.map((t) => ({ pt: t.pt, detail: `${t.questionCount} questions` })),\r\n    [tests],\r\n  );\r\n  const chosen = tests.find((t) => t.pt === pt) ?? null;\r\n  const clock = formatPracticeTestClock(sectionSeconds);\r\n  const beginHintId = React.useId();\r\n\r\n  if (tests.length === 0 && assignedOnly) {\r\n    return (\r\n      <div className="testing-panel">\r\n        <p className="testing-note">\r\n          No practice test has been assigned to you yet. When Joshua assigns\r\n          one, it will be here.\r\n        </p>\r\n      </div>\r\n    );\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  if (tests.length === 0) {\r\n    return (\r\n      <div className="testing-panel">\r\n        <p className="testing-note">\r\n          No PrepTest in the bank currently has the two full Logical Reasoning\r\n          sections and the full Reading Comprehension section a sitting needs.\r\n          Single sections are still available through the other door.\r\n        </p>\r\n      </div>\r\n    );\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  return (\r\n    <div className="testing-panel">\r\n      <div className="testing-field">\r\n        <label htmlFor="testing-pt" id="testing-pt-label">\r\n          PrepTest\r\n        </label>\r\n        <PrepTestCombobox\r\n          emptyMessage="No PrepTest by that number can be sat end to end."\r\n          id="testing-pt"\r\n          labelId="testing-pt-label"\r\n          onChange={setPt}\r\n          options={ptOptions}\r\n          value={pt}\r\n        />\r\n      </div>\r\n\r\n      <div className="testing-field">\r\n        <label htmlFor="testing-pace" id="testing-pace-label">\r\n          Timing\r\n        </label>\r\n        <Select value={pace} onValueChange={(v) => setPace(v as PracticeTestPaceId)}>\r\n          <SelectTrigger\r\n            id="testing-pace"\r\n            aria-labelledby="testing-pace-label testing-pace"\r\n          >\r\n            <SelectValue />\r\n          </SelectTrigger>\r\n          <SelectContent className="testing-menu">\r\n            {paces.map((p) => (\r\n              <SelectItem key={p.id} value={p.id}>\r\n                {p.label}\r\n                {p.sectionSeconds === null\r\n                  ? " · no clock"\r\n                  : ` · ${Math.round(p.sectionSeconds / 60)} min a section`}\r\n              </SelectItem>\r\n            ))}\r\n          </SelectContent>\r\n        </Select>\r\n        {/* The section door has carried this since its timing control was\r\n            built. Without it this door offered accommodations while saying\r\n            nothing about the standing setting those percentages are taken\r\n            from, on the longer of the two commitments. */}\r\n        <p className="testing-note">\r\n          <AccommodationNote baseMinutes={baseMinutes} />\r\n        </p>\r\n      </div>\r\n\r\n      {chosen && (\r\n        <>\r\n          <div className="testing-shape">\r\n            {chosen.legs.map((leg, i) => (\r\n              <div className="testing-shape-row" key={`${leg.kind}-${leg.section}`}>\r\n                <b>Section {i + 1}</b>\r\n                <span>{legLabel(leg as never)}</span>\r\n                <em>\r\n                  {leg.questionCount} questions\r\n                  {clock ? ` · ${clock}` : ""}\r\n                </em>\r\n              </div>\r\n            ))}\r\n          </div>\r\n          <p className="testing-note">\r\n            {chosen.questionCount} scored questions, with a ten-minute break\r\n            after the second section.\r\n          </p>\r\n          {/* The clock is read from the chosen pace rather than printed as\r\n              35:00. It was hard-coded, which stopped being true the moment this\r\n              door started honouring the student\'s own timing mode: an\r\n              accommodated student saw 52:30 on the control and "the real 35:00"\r\n              in the paragraph under it. The clause drops out entirely when\r\n              there is no clock, rather than asserting a number for a sitting\r\n              that is not being timed. */}\r\n          {!chosen.rcFullLength && (\r\n            <p className="testing-note">\r\n              The Reading Comprehension section on this PrepTest runs a few\r\n              questions short, because only questions with a published\r\n              explanation can be drilled. All four passages are there\r\n              {clock ? ` and the clock is the real ${clock}` : ""}, so the\r\n              reading load is unchanged. Your score is simply out of{" "}\r\n              {chosen.questionCount} rather than the high seventies.\r\n            </p>\r\n          )}\r\n        </>\r\n      )}\r\n\r\n      {/* Not `disabled`. A disabled button is skipped by Tab, so a keyboard\r\n          user never meets it and never learns why the door will not open;\r\n          and this room paints it only half a shade lighter, so a sighted\r\n          student pressed it and got nothing. It stays focusable, says it is\r\n          unavailable, and a press on it goes to the field that is missing.\r\n          The line beside it names the missing field for everyone, and is a\r\n          live region so the change is heard when the PrepTest is chosen. */}\r\n      <div className="testing-actions">\r\n        <Button\r\n          aria-disabled={!chosen}\r\n          aria-describedby={chosen ? undefined : beginHintId}\r\n          className="aria-disabled:cursor-not-allowed aria-disabled:opacity-50"\r\n          onClick={() => {\r\n            if (chosen) {\r\n              onBegin(chosen, pace, sectionSeconds);\r\n              return;\r\n            }\r\n            document.getElementById("testing-pt")?.focus();\r\n          }}\r\n        >\r\n          Begin practice test\r\n        </Button>\r\n        <p className="testing-note testing-actions-hint" id={beginHintId} role="status">\r\n          {chosen ? null : "Choose a PrepTest above to begin."}\r\n        </p>\r\n      </div>\r\n    </div>\r\n  );\r\n}\r\n\r\nfunction ResumeList({\r\n  runs,\r\n  onResume,\r\n  onDiscard,\r\n}: {\r\n  runs: PracticeTestRun[];\r\n  onResume: (run: PracticeTestRun) => void;\r\n  onDiscard: (run: PracticeTestRun) => void;\r\n}) {\r\n  // Discard deletes a sitting that can be ninety minutes into a two-hour clock,\r\n  // and it did that on one click with no confirm and no undo. Below 560px\r\n  // .testing-shape-actions goes full width, which puts it exactly where a thumb\r\n  // lands. The confirm is inline rather than a dialog: this room has no modal,\r\n  // and a two-option question that belongs to one row does not need one.\r\n  const [confirming, setConfirming] = React.useState<string | null>(null);\r\n  const keepRef = React.useRef<HTMLButtonElement | null>(null);\r\n\r\n  // Focus the safe option, never the destructive one. A keyboard user who\r\n  // pressed Enter on Discard would otherwise be a single Enter away from the\r\n  // deletion this confirm exists to prevent. Moving focus is also what\r\n  // announces the state change: the group carries the question as its name, so\r\n  // a screen reader reads what is being asked before it reads the choice.\r\n  React.useEffect(() => {\r\n    if (confirming) keepRef.current?.focus();\r\n  }, [confirming]);\r\n\r\n  // A sitting can leave from under an open confirm (resumed in another tab,\r\n  // cleared elsewhere). Without this the row is stuck asking about something\r\n  // that is no longer there.\r\n  React.useEffect(() => {\r\n    if (confirming && !runs.some((run) => run.id === confirming)) {\r\n      setConfirming(null);\r\n    }\r\n  }, [runs, confirming]);\r\n\r\n  if (runs.length === 0) return null;\r\n  return (\r\n    <div className="testing-resume">\r\n      {/* h2, not h3: this is the first section of the page and the only other\r\n          heading is the page title. It sits above the doors because an\r\n          unfinished sitting is the next step, not an afterthought. */}\r\n      <h2>Unfinished sittings</h2>\r\n      <div className="testing-shape">\r\n        {runs.map((run) => {\r\n          return (\r\n            <div className="testing-shape-row" key={run.id}>\r\n              <b>PT {run.pt}</b>\r\n              <span>\r\n                Section {Math.min(run.currentLeg + 1, run.legs.length)} of{" "}\r\n                {run.legs.length}\r\n              </span>\r\n              {/* min-h-[44px] because Button\'s own sm size stops at 40px, and\r\n                  the 44px floor is a defect gate here, not a preference. Both\r\n                  carry a name saying which sitting they act on: two rows of\r\n                  "Resume / Discard" are indistinguishable to a screen reader\r\n                  reading the controls on their own. */}\r\n              {confirming === run.id ? (\r\n                <div\r\n                  className="testing-shape-actions"\r\n                  role="group"\r\n                  aria-label={`Discard the unfinished PrepTest ${run.pt} sitting?`}\r\n                >\r\n                  <span className="testing-confirm">Discard this sitting?</span>\r\n                  <Button\r\n                    size="sm"\r\n                    variant="ghost"\r\n                    className="min-h-[44px]"\r\n                    onClick={() => {\r\n                      onDiscard(run);\r\n                      setConfirming(null);\r\n                    }}\r\n                    aria-label={`Yes, discard the unfinished PrepTest ${run.pt} sitting`}\r\n                  >\r\n                    Discard\r\n                  </Button>\r\n                  <Button\r\n                    ref={keepRef}\r\n                    size="sm"\r\n                    variant="outline"\r\n                    className="min-h-[44px]"\r\n                    onClick={() => setConfirming(null)}\r\n                    aria-label={`Keep the unfinished PrepTest ${run.pt} sitting`}\r\n                  >\r\n                    Keep\r\n                  </Button>\r\n                </div>\r\n              ) : (\r\n                <div className="testing-shape-actions">\r\n                  <Button\r\n                    size="sm"\r\n                    variant="outline"\r\n                    className="min-h-[44px]"\r\n                    onClick={() => onResume(run)}\r\n                    aria-label={`Resume PrepTest ${run.pt}`}\r\n                  >\r\n                    Resume\r\n                  </Button>\r\n                  <Button\r\n                    size="sm"\r\n                    variant="ghost"\r\n                    className="min-h-[44px]"\r\n                    onClick={() => setConfirming(run.id)}\r\n                    aria-label={`Discard the unfinished PrepTest ${run.pt} sitting`}\r\n                  >\r\n                    Discard\r\n                  </Button>\r\n                </div>\r\n              )}\r\n            </div>\r\n          );\r\n        })}\r\n      </div>\r\n    </div>\r\n  );\r\n}\r\n\r\n/**\r\n * The PrepTests this student was assigned. Only read for a student without the\r\n * Practice tests switch: with it, the whole bank is theirs. Without it, the\r\n * room holds exactly what Joshua assigned, one sitting per assignment.\r\n */\r\nfunction useAssignedPrepTests(enabled: boolean, userId: string | undefined) {\r\n  return useQuery<number[]>({\r\n    queryKey: ["assigned-preptests", userId],\r\n    enabled: enabled && !!userId,\r\n    queryFn: async () => {\r\n      const { data, error } = await supabase\r\n        .from("practice_test_assignments" as never)\r\n        .select("pt")\r\n        .eq("student_id", userId!)\r\n        .neq("status", "archived");\r\n      if (error) throw error;\r\n      return ((data ?? []) as Array<{ pt: number }>).map((row) => row.pt);\r\n    },\r\n  });\r\n}\r\n\r\nexport default function TestingCentre() {\r\n  const navigate = useNavigate();\r\n  const { manifest, isLoading, error } = useQuestionBank();\r\n  const { user } = useAuth();\r\n  const permissions = useUserPermissions();\r\n  const assignedOnly = !permissions.loading && !permissions.is_admin && !permissions.has_practice_access;\r\n  const { data: assignedPts } = useAssignedPrepTests(assignedOnly, user?.id);\r\n  // An assigned-only student has one door: the sittings they were given. The\r\n  // section door runs through Drill, which is a switch they do not have.\r\n  const [door, setDoor] = React.useState<Door>(null);\r\n  React.useEffect(() => {\r\n    if (assignedOnly) setDoor("test");\r\n  }, [assignedOnly]);\r\n  const { sections: rcSections, error: rcError } = useRcSections(door === "test");\r\n  const [runs, setRuns] = React.useState<PracticeTestRun[]>([]);\r\n\r\n  React.useEffect(() => {\r\n    setRuns(listPracticeTestRuns().filter((r) => r.status === "in-progress"));\r\n  }, []);\r\n\r\n  const tests = React.useMemo(() => {\r\n    const all = manifest && rcSections ? buildPracticeTests(manifest, rcSections) : [];\r\n    if (!assignedOnly) return all;\r\n    const allowed = new Set(assignedPts ?? []);\r\n    return all.filter((t) => allowed.has(t.pt));\r\n  }, [manifest, rcSections, assignedOnly, assignedPts]);\r\n\r\n  const beginTest = (\r\n    blueprint: PracticeTestBlueprint,\r\n    pace: PracticeTestPaceId,\r\n    sectionSeconds: number | null,\r\n  ) => {\r\n    const run = createPracticeTestRun(blueprint, pace, sectionSeconds);\r\n    navigate(practiceTestPath(run.id));\r\n  };\r\n\r\n  const discard = (run: PracticeTestRun) => {\r\n    deletePracticeTestRun(run.id);\r\n    setRuns((current) => current.filter((r) => r.id !== run.id));\r\n  };\r\n\r\n  // A door\'s panel renders below the two door buttons, which on a laptop puts it\r\n  // under the fold: the click looks like it did nothing, and the section type\r\n  // picker (Logical Reasoning / Reading Comprehension) is never seen, so the door\r\n  // reads as LR-only. Bring the panel to the student when it opens.\r\n  //\r\n  // Anchored on the doors rather than the panel. Scrolling the panel itself to\r\n  // the top of the viewport over-corrected into the opposite failure: the panel\r\n  // is taller than the viewport, so the page ran to its own scroll maximum and\r\n  // took the room\'s name and the door that was just pressed with it. Measured at\r\n  // 1280x900, .classroom-title landed at -234px and 78% of the door row was off\r\n  // screen, at the moment a student commits to a timed section. Putting the door\r\n  // row at the top keeps the choice that was just made on screen, and the panel\r\n  // begins directly beneath it.\r\n  const panelRef = React.useRef<HTMLDivElement | null>(null);\r\n  const doorsRef = React.useRef<HTMLDivElement | null>(null);\r\n  React.useEffect(() => {\r\n    if (!door) return;\r\n    const panel = panelRef.current;\r\n    if (!panel) return;\r\n    // Leave the page alone when the panel already starts on screen. The click\r\n    // did not read as a no-op, and moving the page under a student who can\r\n    // already see the result is its own small betrayal.\r\n    const top = panel.getBoundingClientRect().top;\r\n    if (top >= 0 && top <= window.innerHeight - PANEL_VISIBLE_MARGIN) return;\r\n    const prefersReducedMotion = window.matchMedia?.(\r\n      "(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)",\r\n    ).matches;\r\n    (doorsRef.current ?? panel).scrollIntoView({\r\n      behavior: prefersReducedMotion ? "auto" : "smooth",\r\n      block: "start",\r\n    });\r\n  }, [door]);\r\n\r\n  return (\r\n    <>\r\n      <h1 className="classroom-title">Testing Centre</h1>\r\n      <p className="testing-lede">\r\n        Official LSAT material, timed like test day.\r\n      </p>\r\n\r\n      <ResumeList\r\n        runs={runs}\r\n        onResume={(run) => navigate(practiceTestPath(run.id))}\r\n        onDiscard={discard}\r\n      />\r\n\r\n      {/* The doors carry a title and two supporting lines, and those used to be\r\n          an h2 and a p. Neither is legal inside a button — its content model is\r\n          phrasing only — and the h2s put two headings in the outline that a\r\n          screen reader lands on as controls. They are spans that look the same.\r\n\r\n          aria-expanded, not aria-pressed: these are not toggle buttons, they\r\n          disclose a panel, and aria-controls is what says which panel. */}\r\n      <div className="testing-doors" ref={doorsRef}>\r\n        {!assignedOnly && (\r\n        <button\r\n          type="button"\r\n          className="testing-door"\r\n          aria-expanded={door === "section"}\r\n          aria-controls={PANEL_ID}\r\n          onClick={() => setDoor(door === "section" ? null : "section")}\r\n        >\r\n          <span className="testing-door-title">Take a section</span>\r\n          {/* No clock here: the meta line below already carries it. */}\r\n          <span className="testing-door-body">\r\n            One real LR or RC section from an official PrepTest.\r\n          </span>\r\n          <span className="testing-door-meta">One section · 35 minutes</span>\r\n        </button>\r\n        )}\r\n\r\n        <button\r\n          type="button"\r\n          className="testing-door"\r\n          aria-expanded={door === "test"}\r\n          aria-controls={PANEL_ID}\r\n          onClick={() => setDoor(door === "test" ? null : "test")}\r\n        >\r\n          <span className="testing-door-title">Take a practice test</span>\r\n          <span className="testing-door-body">\r\n            Three scored sections from one PrepTest, in order, with a break.\r\n          </span>\r\n          <span className="testing-door-meta">Three sections · about 2 hours</span>\r\n        </button>\r\n      </div>\r\n\r\n      <div id={PANEL_ID} ref={panelRef}>\r\n        {door === "section" && (\r\n          <div className="testing-panel">\r\n            {isLoading && <Working>Loading questions…</Working>}\r\n            {!isLoading && error && <p className="testing-note">{error}</p>}\r\n            {!isLoading && !error && manifest && (\r\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '### 2\n-- Practice-test runs live in the database, and legs are tagged rather than guessed.\r\n--\r\n-- Until now a sitting existed only in one browser\'s localStorage. Clearing site\r\n-- data lost it, a second device could not see it, and the scorer found a leg by\r\n-- matching "this user, this section, finished after the leg began", which is a\r\n-- guess that gets less true the more a student drills.\r\n--\r\n-- This migration is the schema half of that change. It is written to converge\r\n-- from any starting point: every statement is guarded, so a clean replay and a\r\n-- re-run against the live database both end at the same schema.\r\n--\r\n-- ORDER IS LOAD-BEARING in 1a. `alter table ... add column ... default` fills\r\n-- every existing row with the default, so adding the default before the backfill\r\n-- would overwrite real `section_mode` values and real completion times. Add the\r\n-- columns bare, backfill from the truth already in the row, and only then attach\r\n-- the defaults for future inserts.\r\n\r\n-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\r\n-- 1a. Normalize section_history\r\n-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\r\n\r\nalter table public.section_history\r\n  add column if not exists mode text,\r\n  add column if not exists completed_at timestamptz,\r\n  add column if not exists user_id uuid references auth.users (id) on delete cascade;\r\n\r\nupdate public.section_history\r\n   set mode = coalesce(mode, section_mode, \'full-section\')\r\n where mode is null;\r\n\r\nupdate public.section_history\r\n   set completed_at = created_at\r\n where completed_at is null;\r\n\r\nalter table public.section_history alter column mode set default \'full-section\';\r\nalter table public.section_history alter column completed_at set default now();\r\nalter table public.section_history alter column section_mode drop not null;\r\n\r\n-- The INSERT policy has to be REPLACED, not supplemented. PostgreSQL ORs\r\n-- permissive policies together, so leaving the old class-only rule in place\r\n-- would keep a live bypass around the new ownership test, and the composite FK\r\n-- further down would be decorative. The legacy policies are dropped by role\r\n-- rather than by name because their names differ across environments; this\r\n-- drops exactly the INSERT policies (\'a\' = ALTER/append = INSERT in pg_policy)\r\n-- and leaves SELECT/UPDATE/DELETE untouched.\r\ndo $$\r\ndeclare\r\n  pol record;\r\nbegin\r\n  for pol in\r\n    select polname\r\n      from pg_policy\r\n     where polrelid = \'public.section_history\'::regclass\r\n       and polcmd = \'a\'\r\n  loop\r\n    execute format(\r\n      \'drop policy if exists %I on public.section_history\', pol.polname\r\n    );\r\n  end loop;\r\nend $$;\r\n\r\ncreate policy "section_history_owner_insert" on public.section_history\r\n  for insert with check (\r\n    user_id = auth.uid()\r\n    and class_id = public.get_user_class_id(auth.uid())\r\n  );\r\n\r\n-- If this returns anything other than one row, an insert path was reintroduced\r\n-- somewhere and the ownership guarantee is gone:\r\n--   select polname from pg_policy\r\n--    where polrelid = \'public.section_history\'::regclass and polcmd = \'a\';\r\n\r\n-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\r\n-- 1b. The runs table\r\n-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\r\n\r\n-- id is text, not uuid: newRunId()\'s fallback path does not produce a uuid.\r\ncreate table if not exists public.practice_test_runs (\r\n  id               text primary key,\r\n  user_id          uuid not null default auth.uid() references auth.users (id) on delete cascade,\r\n  pt               int not null,\r\n  pace             text not null,\r\n  legs             jsonb not null,\r\n  current_leg      int not null default 0,\r\n  break_after_leg  int not null,\r\n  break_taken      boolean not null default false,\r\n  break_started_at timestamptz,\r\n  status           text not null check (status in (\'in-progress\', \'complete\', \'abandoned\')),\r\n  revision         int not null default 0,\r\n  completed_at     timestamptz,\r\n  raw_correct      int,\r\n  raw_total        int,\r\n  scaled_score     int,\r\n  score_source     text check (score_source in (\'official-table\', \'estimate\')),\r\n  created_at       timestamptz not null default now(),\r\n  updated_at       timestamptz not null default now(),\r\n  -- Exists solely as the target of the composite FKs below. It is what makes\r\n  -- cross-user leg attribution impossible at the schema level rather than by\r\n  -- convention.\r\n  unique (id, user_id)\r\n);\r\n\r\ncreate index if not exists practice_test_runs_user_status\r\n  on public.practice_test_runs (user_id, status);\r\n\r\nalter table public.practice_test_runs enable row level security;\r\n\r\n-- No set_updated_at / handle_updated_at function exists in this project\'s\r\n-- migrations, so it is created here rather than reused.\r\ncreate or replace function public.touch_practice_test_runs()\r\nreturns trigger\r\nlanguage plpgsql\r\nset search_path = public\r\nas $$\r\nbegin\r\n  new.updated_at = now();\r\n  return new;\r\nend;\r\n$$;\r\n\r\ndrop trigger if exists practice_test_runs_touch on public.practice_test_runs;\r\ncreate trigger practice_test_runs_touch\r\n  before update on public.practice_test_runs\r\n  for each row execute function public.touch_practice_test_runs();\r\n\r\ndrop policy if exists "pt_runs_owner_select" on public.practice_test_runs;\r\ncreate policy "pt_runs_owner_select" on public.practice_test_runs\r\n  for select using (user_id = auth.uid());\r\n\r\ndrop policy if exists "pt_runs_owner_insert" on public.practice_test_runs;\r\ncreate policy "pt_runs_owner_insert" on public.practice_test_runs\r\n  for insert with check (user_id = auth.uid());\r\n\r\ndrop policy if exists "pt_runs_owner_update" on public.practice_test_runs;\r\ncreate policy "pt_runs_owner_update" on public.practice_test_runs\r\n  for update using (user_id = auth.uid()) with check (user_id = auth.uid());\r\n\r\ndrop policy if exists "pt_runs_admin_select" on public.practice_test_runs;\r\ncreate policy "pt_runs_admin_select" on public.practice_test_runs\r\n  for select using (public.has_role(auth.uid(), \'admin\'));\r\n\r\n-- Deliberately NO delete policy, for anyone. A sitting is a record of work.\r\n-- Terminal-state protection beyond RLS is enforced client-side by\r\n-- status-filtered conditional updates; a hostile owner editing their own row\r\n-- can corrupt only their own history, which is acceptable here.\r\n\r\n-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\r\n-- 1c. Leg attribution\r\n-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\r\n\r\nalter table public.section_history\r\n  add column if not exists practice_test_run_id text,\r\n  add column if not exists practice_test_leg int;\r\n\r\nalter table public.rc_drill_sessions\r\n'}]

## assistant

The database already has a `practice_test_runs` schema with owner policies and a revision field, but the current Testing Centre UI still lists only local browser runs. I’m checking the existing client wiring next so the proposed migration does not duplicate a partial implementation.

"Return to" : "Begin"} section {run.currentLeg + 1}\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '### 2\n/**\r\n * Resume state for every OTHER drill — Smart Drill, custom type drills, and\r\n * full sections.\r\n *\r\n * Guided drills and Intro to LR practice sets already survive a closed tab, a\r\n * sign-out, or a different device (see guidedDrillSession). Nothing else did:\r\n * a student mid-way through a Smart Drill who closed the browser came back to\r\n * a fresh shuffle, and the answers they had already given were nowhere on the\r\n * screen. The attempts themselves were never lost — those are written to the\r\n * `attempts` table per question — but the run was, which reads as lost work.\r\n *\r\n * This rides the exact same store as the other two, namespaced so the three can\r\n * never collide:\r\n *\r\n *     practice set   →  pool_slug "mc-practice-1"\r\n *     guided drill   →  pool_slug "guided::<assignmentId>"\r\n *     bootcamp day   →  pool_slug "drill::bootcamp-day::<day>::<part>"\r\n *     homework       →  pool_slug "drill::assignment::<assignmentId>"\r\n *     everything else→  pool_slug "drill::<mode>::<fingerprint>"\r\n *\r\n * Failure policy, inherited: reads degrade to null (a missing resume row must\r\n * never block starting a drill), writes surface to the caller.\r\n */\r\n\r\nimport {\r\n  loadPracticeSession,\r\n  savePracticeSessionSnapshot,\r\n  completePracticeSession,\r\n  type PracticeSessionSnapshot,\r\n} from \'@/bootcamps/main-conclusion/lib/practiceSessionStore\';\r\nimport type { PracticeSession } from \'@/bootcamps/main-conclusion/persistence/records\';\r\n\r\nexport const DRILL_SESSION_PREFIX = \'drill::\';\r\n\r\nexport type DrillResumeSession = PracticeSession;\r\nexport type DrillResumeSnapshot = PracticeSessionSnapshot;\r\n\r\nexport type DrillResumeIdentity = {\r\n  kind: \'bootcamp-day\';\r\n  day: number;\r\n  part: \'homework\' | \'practice\';\r\n};\r\n\r\n/** Short, stable, filename-safe digest of whatever identifies the run. */\r\nfunction fingerprint(input: string): string {\r\n  let h = 2166136261;\r\n  for (let i = 0; i < input.length; i++) {\r\n    h ^= input.charCodeAt(i);\r\n    h = Math.imul(h, 16777619);\r\n  }\r\n  return (h >>> 0).toString(36);\r\n}\r\n\r\n/**\r\n * Key one run\'s resume row.\r\n *\r\n * A homework assignment is identified by the assignment: two assignments of\r\n * the same section are two sittings, and a row named by the section alone let\r\n * the second one open on the first one\'s answers and the first one\'s spent\r\n * clock. Otherwise a full section is identified by the section itself; a\r\n * filtered drill by the filters that drew it; Smart Drill by nothing but its\r\n * mode, because it has a single continuous run per student. Returns null for\r\n * the surfaces that own their own resume rows (guided, practice sets) and for\r\n * anything launched from an explicit list of qids, where the caller already\r\n * knows the set.\r\n */\r\nexport function drillResumeKey(input: {\r\n  mode: string;\r\n  config?: unknown;\r\n  identity?: DrillResumeIdentity;\r\n  /** The classroom homework assignment this run belongs to, when it does. */\r\n  assignmentId?: string | null;\r\n}): string | null {\r\n  if (input.assignmentId) {\r\n    return `${DRILL_SESSION_PREFIX}assignment::${input.assignmentId}`;\r\n  }\r\n  if (input.identity?.kind === \'bootcamp-day\') {\r\n    return `${DRILL_SESSION_PREFIX}bootcamp-day::${input.identity.day}::${input.identity.part}`;\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  const mode = input.mode;\r\n  if (mode === \'adaptive\') return `${DRILL_SESSION_PREFIX}adaptive`;\r\n\r\n  const config = (input.config ?? null) as Record<string, unknown> | null;\r\n\r\n  if (mode === \'full-section\') {\r\n    if (!config) return null;\r\n    const pt = config.pt;\r\n    const section = config.section;\r\n    if (pt == null || section == null) return null;\r\n    return `${DRILL_SESSION_PREFIX}section::pt${pt}-s${section}`;\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  if (mode === \'type-drill\' || mode === \'natural-drill\') {\r\n    if (!config) return null;\r\n    const selected = config.selectedQids as string[] | undefined;\r\n    if (selected?.length) {\r\n      // A pre-selected set is its own identity: the same list re-opened is the\r\n      // same run, a different list is a different one.\r\n      return `${DRILL_SESSION_PREFIX}${mode}::set-${fingerprint([...selected].sort().join(\',\'))}`;\r\n    }\r\n    const shape = JSON.stringify({\r\n      qtypes: config.qtypes ?? [],\r\n      difficulties: config.difficulties ?? [],\r\n      pts: config.pts ?? [],\r\n      sections: config.sections ?? [],\r\n      qfeatures: config.qfeatures ?? [],\r\n      reasoningTypes: config.reasoningTypes ?? [],\r\n      searchQuery: config.searchQuery ?? \'\',\r\n      questionNumber: config.questionNumber ?? null,\r\n      count: config.count ?? null,\r\n    });\r\n    return `${DRILL_SESSION_PREFIX}${mode}::${fingerprint(shape)}`;\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  return null;\r\n}\r\n\r\n/** The stored run for this drill, or null when there is nothing to resume. */\r\nexport async function loadDrillResumeSession(\r\n  userId: string,\r\n  key: string | null,\r\n  legacyKey?: string | null,\r\n): Promise<DrillResumeSession | null> {\r\n  if (!userId || !key) return null;\r\n  const current = await loadPracticeSession(userId, key);\r\n  if (current || !legacyKey || legacyKey === key) return current;\r\n  return loadPracticeSession(userId, legacyKey);\r\n}\r\n\r\n/** Write where the student is right now (local-first, debounced cloud upsert). */\r\nexport function saveDrillResumeSnapshot(\r\n  userId: string,\r\n  key: string | null,\r\n  snapshot: DrillResumeSnapshot,\r\n): Promise<void> {\r\n  if (!userId || !key) return Promise.resolve();\r\n  return savePracticeSessionSnapshot(userId, { slug: key, completionId: key }, snapshot);\r\n}\r\n\r\n/**\r\n * Mark the run finished. Nothing is deleted — the queue and every answer stay\r\n * in the row for review — it simply stops being offered as a resume, so the\r\n * next launch of the same drill draws fresh questions.\r\n */\r\nexport function completeDrillResumeSession(\r\n  userId: string,\r\n  key: string | null,\r\n  snapshot: DrillResumeSnapshot,\r\n): Promise<void> {\r\n  if (!userId || !key) return Promise.resolve();\r\n  return completePracticeSession(userId, { slug: key, completionId: key }, snapshot);\r\n}\r\n\r\n/**\r\n * Is this stored row worth resuming?\r\n *\r\n * Unlike a guided drill, these runs are drawn fresh on every launch, so there\r\n * is no fixed set to compare against — the stored queue IS the run. It is\r\n * resumable when it is still in progress and the student actually answered\r\n * something; a row with no answers has nothing to restore and would only\r\n * freeze a stale shuffle in place.\r\n */\r\nexport function isResumableDrillSession(stored: DrillResumeSession | null): boolean {\r\n  return (\r\n    !!stored &&\r\n    stored.status === \'in_progress\' &&\r\n    stored.question_queue.length > 0 &&\r\n    stored.attempts.length > 0\r\n  );\r\n}\r\n\r\n/**\r\n * Was this row\'s section clock already spent when it was stored?\r\n *\r\n * A row written at or after the buzzer carries elapsed_ms equal to the budget\r\n * (the countdown reports zero, so the spend is the whole budget). Restoring it\r\n * as an ordinary resume opens the section on 00:00 with no buzzer, because the\r\n * regulation mark it also carries tells the buzzer effect it has already\r\n * asked. The runner uses this to open such a row on the buzzer instead: the\r\n * answers are kept, the clock is not handed back, and the student is asked to\r\n * finish and score the section as it was sat.\r\n *\r\n * Untimed runs have no budget and are never "spent".\r\n */\r\nexport function isSpentDrillSession(\r\n  stored: DrillResumeSession | null,\r\n  budgetMs: number | undefined,\r\n): boolean {\r\n  if (!stored || !budgetMs || !Number.isFinite(budgetMs)) return false;\r\n  return (stored.elapsed_ms ?? 0) >= budgetMs;\r\n}\r\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '### 3\n\r\nsrc/pages\\AcademyMainhub.tsx:128:     returns before `useQuestionBank`\'s loading gate — waiting on 5.5 MB of LR\r\nsrc/pages\\AcademyMainhub.tsx:158:              onClick={() => window.location.reload()}\r\nsrc/pages\\AcademyMainhub.tsx:161:              Reload the question bank\r\nsrc/pages\\AcademyMainhub.tsx:238:    (flag: PermissionFlag) => perms.loading || perms.is_admin || !!perms[flag],\r\nsrc/pages\\AcademyMainhub.tsx:295:     moment the passage index is loading and then lands in the drill; they\r\nsrc/pages\\AcademyMainhub.section.test.tsx:14:vi.mock("@/integrations/supabase/client", () => ({\r\nsrc/pages\\AcademyMainhub.section.test.tsx:15:  supabase: {\r\nsrc/pages\\AcademyMainhub.section.test.tsx:26:    loading: false,\r\nsrc/pages\\AcademyMainhub.section.test.tsx:85:  useInbox: () => ({ conversations: [], loading: false, unreadCount: 0, \r\nrefresh: vi.fn() }),\r\nsrc/pages\\AcademyMainhub.section.test.tsx:86:  useConversationMessages: () => ({ messages: [], loading: false, \r\nrefresh: vi.fn() }),\r\nsrc/pages\\AcademyMainhub.section.test.tsx:97:    loading: false,\r\nsrc/pages\\AcademyMainhub.section.test.tsx:106:  useStudentAssignments: () => ({ assignments: [], loading: false, \r\nerror: null, refresh: vi.fn() }),\r\nsrc/pages\\AcademyMainhub.section.test.tsx:107:  useStudentTAAssignments: () => ({ assignments: [], loading: false, \r\nerror: null, refresh: vi.fn() }),\r\nsrc/pages\\AcademyMainhub.section.test.tsx:125:    <div>Account menu {displayName ?? ]; const rs=await Promise.allSettled(cmds.map(cmd=>tools.exec_command({cmd,workdir:"C:\\Users\\Joshu\\dev\\ai-os\\projects\\lsatu-wt-drill-layout",max_output_tokens:17000}))); rs.forEach((r,i)=>text(`### ${i}\n${r.status==="fulfilled"?r.value.output:r.reason}`));

[{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 2.5 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': "### 0\n  return run.sectionSeconds !== undefined\r\n    ? run.sectionSeconds\r\n    : practiceTestSectionSeconds(run.pace);\r\n}\r\n\r\nexport type PracticeTestLegKind = 'lr' | 'rc';\r\n\r\nexport interface PracticeTestLeg {\r\n  kind: PracticeTestLegKind;\r\n  /** The section number it carried on the real PrepTest. */\r\n  section: number;\r\n  /** Questions in this leg — what it contributes to the raw score. */\r\n  questionCount: number;\r\n  /** RC only: the four passages, in published order. */\r\n  passageIds?: number[];\r\n  /** ISO stamp set when the student begins this leg. */\r\n  startedAt?: string;\r\n  /** ISO stamp set when its result is read back. */\r\n  completedAt?: string;\r\n  correct?: number;\r\n  answered?: number;\r\n  timeMs?: number;\r\n}\r\n\r\nexport type PracticeTestStatus = 'in-progress' | 'complete' | 'abandoned';\r\n\r\nexport interface PracticeTestRun {\r\n  id: string;\r\n  pt: number;\r\n  pace: PracticeTestPaceId;\r\n  /**\r\n   * Seconds per section for this sitting, resolved against the student's own\r\n   * standard clock when the run was created. Optional because runs stored\r\n   * before it existed do not carry it; read it through\r\n   * `practiceTestRunSectionSeconds`, never directly.\r\n   */\r\n  sectionSeconds?: number | null;\r\n  legs: PracticeTestLeg[];\r\n  /** Index of the leg the student is on. Equals legs.length when finished. */\r\n  currentLeg: number;\r\n  /** Legs completed before the break. The real test breaks after two. */\r\n  breakAfterLeg: number;\r\n  /** True once the student has taken (or skipped) the break. */\r\n  breakTaken: boolean;\r\n  /** ISO stamp set when the break clock started, so it survives a reload. */\r\n  breakStartedAt?: string;\r\n  status: PracticeTestStatus;\r\n  createdAt: string;\r\n}\r\n\r\n/** Earlier PT118 sittings included experimental S3 and an extra S1 item. */\r\nexport function hasOutdatedPt118Structure(run: PracticeTestRun): boolean {\r\n  if (run.pt !== 118) return false;\r\n  const legs = run.legs.map((leg) => `${leg.kind}:${leg.section}:${leg.questionCount}`);\r\n  return legs.join(',') !== 'lr:1:25,rc:2:27,lr:4:25';\r\n"}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': "### 1\n  return `${STORE_PREFIX}${id}`;\r\n}\r\n\r\nfunction newRunId(): string {\r\n  const c = globalThis.crypto;\r\n  if (c && 'randomUUID' in c) return c.randomUUID();\r\n  return `pt-${Date.now()}-${Math.floor(Math.random() * 1e6)}`;\r\n}\r\n\r\nexport function createPracticeTestRun(\r\n  blueprint: PracticeTestBlueprint,\r\n  pace: PracticeTestPaceId,\r\n  /**\r\n   * Seconds per section, already resolved against the student's own clock.\r\n   * Stored on the run so the sitting keeps the clock it started with even if\r\n   * the timing setting changes while it is in progress.\r\n   */\r\n  sectionSeconds: number | null = practiceTestSectionSeconds(pace),\r\n): PracticeTestRun {\r\n  const run: PracticeTestRun = {\r\n    id: newRunId(),\r\n    pt: blueprint.pt,\r\n    pace,\r\n    sectionSeconds,\r\n    legs: blueprint.legs.map((l) => ({ ...l })),\r\n    currentLeg: 0,\r\n    // Two sections, then the break — the shape of the real sitting. A test that\r\n    // somehow has fewer legs breaks after the second-to-last rather than never.\r\n    breakAfterLeg: Math.min(2, Math.max(1, blueprint.legs.length - 1)),\r\n    breakTaken: false,\r\n    status: 'in-progress',\r\n    createdAt: new Date().toISOString(),\r\n  };\r\n  savePracticeTestRun(run);\r\n  return run;\r\n}\r\n\r\nexport function savePracticeTestRun(run: PracticeTestRun): void {\r\n  try {\r\n    localStorage.setItem(storeKey(run.id), JSON.stringify(run));\r\n  } catch {\r\n    // A full or disabled store must not take the sitting down. The student\r\n    // keeps answering; only resume-after-reload is lost.\r\n  }\r\n}\r\n\r\nexport function loadPracticeTestRun(id: string): PracticeTestRun | null {\r\n  try {\r\n    const raw = localStorage.getItem(storeKey(id));\r\n    if (!raw) return null;\r\n    const parsed = JSON.parse(raw) as PracticeTestRun;\r\n    if (!parsed || !Array.isArray(parsed.legs)) return null;\r\n    return parsed;\r\n  } catch {\r\n    return null;\r\n  }\r\n}\r\n\r\nexport function listPracticeTestRuns(): PracticeTestRun[] {\r\n  const runs: PracticeTestRun[] = [];\r\n  try {\r\n    for (let i = 0; i < localStorage.length; i += 1) {\r\n      const key = localStorage.key(i);\r\n      if (!key || !key.startsWith(STORE_PREFIX)) continue;\r\n      const run = loadPracticeTestRun(key.slice(STORE_PREFIX.length));\r\n      if (run) runs.push(run);\r\n    }\r\n  } catch {\r\n    return [];\r\n  }\r\n  return runs.sort((a, b) => b.createdAt.localeCompare(a.createdAt));\r\n}\r\n\r\nexport function deletePracticeTestRun(id: string): void {\r\n  try {\r\n    localStorage.removeItem(storeKey(id));\r\n  } catch {\r\n    /* nothing to clean up */\r\n  }\r\n}\r\n\r\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\r\n/* Derived views                                                               */\r\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\r\n\r\n"}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '### 2\n/**\r\n * Practice-set resume store for the Intro to LR practice drills.\r\n *\r\n * A student who starts a practice set and leaves — closes the tab, clicks away\r\n * to a lesson, signs out, comes back tomorrow on another machine — must land\r\n * back on the question they stopped on, with every answer they already gave\r\n * still marked. React state and URL params cannot do that: both die with the\r\n * page. So each practice set keeps one durable row per user in the shared\r\n * bootcamp_kv persistence (the same layer module_progress uses), written on\r\n * every answer and on every question move.\r\n *\r\n * These are standalone functions rather than a hook because the drill runner\r\n * (src/pages/Drill.tsx) lives outside the bootcamp\'s React tree — the same\r\n * reason recordPracticeCompletion.ts exists.\r\n *\r\n * Failure policy: reads degrade to null (a missing resume row must never block\r\n * starting a drill). Writes throw so the caller can surface a toast rather than\r\n * silently losing a student\'s place.\r\n */\r\n\r\nimport { getPersistence } from \'@/bootcamps/main-conclusion/persistence/factory\';\r\nimport { TABLES, PracticeSession, type PracticeSessionAttempt } from \'@/bootcamps/main-conclusion/persistence/records\';\r\nimport { now } from \'@/bootcamps/main-conclusion/lib/ids\';\r\nimport {\r\n  MAIN_CONCLUSION_PRACTICE_POOLS,\r\n  type PracticePool,\r\n} from \'@/bootcamps/main-conclusion/data/practiceDrillPools\';\r\n\r\nexport const PRACTICE_SESSION_DATA_VERSION = 2;\r\n\r\nconst PT118_S1_ANSWERS: Record<number, string> = {\r\n  1: \'A\', 2: \'C\', 3: \'B\', 4: \'C\', 6: \'D\', 7: \'B\', 8: \'A\',\r\n  9: \'E\', 10: \'A\', 11: \'C\', 12: \'C\', 13: \'E\', 14: \'E\',\r\n  15: \'E\', 16: \'C\', 17: \'B\', 18: \'D\', 19: \'A\', 20: \'D\',\r\n  21: \'A\', 22: \'E\', 23: \'C\', 24: \'B\', 25: \'C\', 26: \'E\',\r\n};\r\n\r\nfunction migratePt118Qid(qid: string): string | null {\r\n  const match = /^PT118-S([1234])-Q(\\d+)$/.exec(qid);\r\n  if (!match) return qid;\r\n  const section = Number(match[1]);\r\n  const number = Number(match[2]);\r\n  if (section === 3 && number === 22) return null;\r\n  if (section === 1 && number >= 5 && number <= 25) return `PT118-S1-Q${number + 1}`;\r\n  return qid;\r\n}\r\n\r\nfunction pt118CorrectAnswer(qid: string): string | undefined {\r\n  const match = /^PT118-S([12])-Q(\\d+)$/.exec(qid);\r\n  if (!match) return undefined;\r\n  const section = Number(match[1]);\r\n  const number = Number(match[2]);\r\n  if (section === 1) return PT118_S1_ANSWERS[number];\r\n  if (section === 2) return ({ 1: \'D\', 5: \'A\' } as Record<number, string>)[number];\r\n  return undefined;\r\n}\r\n\r\n/** Remap pre-repair PT118 resume IDs once, preserving the student\'s place by content. */\r\nexport function migratePracticeSession(row: PracticeSession): PracticeSession {\r\n  if ((row.data_version ?? 0) >= PRACTICE_SESSION_DATA_VERSION) return row;\r\n  // These namespaces are written by the Drill runner, whose displayed LR\r\n  // content comes from the local JSON QuestionBank. An unknown producer may\r\n  // have used the already-canonical catalog, so its IDs are not safe to shift.\r\n  if (!row.pool_slug.startsWith(\'drill::\') && !row.pool_slug.startsWith(\'guided::\')) return row;\r\n  const oldCurrent = row.question_queue[row.current_index];\r\n  const mappedCurrent = oldCurrent ? migratePt118Qid(oldCurrent) : null;\r\n  const seen = new Set<string>();\r\n  const questionQueue = row.question_queue.flatMap((qid) => {\r\n    const mapped = migratePt118Qid(qid);\r\n    if (!mapped || seen.has(mapped)) return [];\r\n    seen.add(mapped);\r\n    return [mapped];\r\n  });\r\n  const seenAttempts = new Set<string>();\r\n  const attempts = row.attempts.flatMap((attempt) => {\r\n    const qid = migratePt118Qid(attempt.qid);\r\n    if (!qid || seenAttempts.has(qid)) return [];\r\n    seenAttempts.add(qid);\r\n    const answer = pt118CorrectAnswer(qid);\r\n    return [{ ...attempt, qid, correct: answer ? attempt.selected_answer === answer : attempt.correct }];\r\n  });\r\n  const currentIndex = mappedCurrent && questionQueue.includes(mappedCurrent)\r\n    ? questionQueue.indexOf(mappedCurrent)\r\n    : Math.min(row.current_index, Math.max(questionQueue.length - 1, 0));\r\n  const regulationAnsweredQids = row.regulation_answered_qids && [\r\n    ...new Set(row.regulation_answered_qids.map(migratePt118Qid).filter((qid): qid is string => !!qid)),\r\n  ];\r\n  return PracticeSession.parse({\r\n    ...row,\r\n    data_version: PRACTICE_SESSION_DATA_VERSION,\r\n    question_queue: questionQueue,\r\n    current_index: currentIndex,\r\n    attempts,\r\n    regulation_answered_qids: regulationAnsweredQids,\r\n  });\r\n}\r\n\r\n/**\r\n * Deterministic per-user, per-pool record id, so every read and write lands on\r\n * the same row without a lookup. Mirrors progressIdFor\'s FNV-1a approach but\r\n * hashes user + slug together.\r\n */\r\nexport function practiceSessionIdFor(userId: string, poolSlug: string): string {\r\n  const input = `${userId}::${poolSlug}`;\r\n  let h = 0x811c9dc5;\r\n  for (let i = 0; i < input.length; i++) {\r\n    h ^= input.charCodeAt(i);\r\n    h = Math.imul(h, 0x01000193);\r\n  }\r\n  let h2 = 0x811c9dc5;\r\n  for (let i = input.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {\r\n    h2 ^= input.charCodeAt(i);\r\n    h2 = Math.imul(h2, 0x01000193);\r\n  }\r\n  const hex = (h >>> 0).toString(16).padStart(8, \'0\');\r\n  const hex2 = (h2 >>> 0).toString(16).padStart(8, \'0\');\r\n  return `00000000-0000-4000-8000-${hex}${hex2.slice(0, 4)}`;\r\n}\r\n\r\n/**\r\n * One writer per row at a time.\r\n *\r\n * Both writers below read the row and then write it, with an await in between.\r\n * The drill runner fires a snapshot on every answer and a completion at the\r\n * end, and nothing stopped a snapshot that had already read the row from\r\n * writing it AFTER the completion had — which put a finished section back to\r\n * in_progress. Writes to different rows still overlap.\r\n */\r\nconst rowChains = new Map<string, Promise<unknown>>();\r\n\r\nasync function withRow<T>(id: string, fn: () => Promise<T>): Promise<T> {\r\n  const previous = rowChains.get(id) ?? Promise.resolve();\r\n  const run = previous.then(fn, fn);\r\n  rowChains.set(id, run.catch(() => undefined));\r\n  try {\r\n    return await run;\r\n  } finally {\r\n    if (rowChains.get(id) === run) rowChains.delete(id);\r\n  }\r\n}\r\n\r\nexport interface PracticeSessionSnapshot {\r\n  questionQueue: string[];\r\n  currentIndex: number;\r\n  attempts: PracticeSessionAttempt[];\r\n  /** Section clock already spent, for the surfaces that run one. */\r\n  elapsedMs?: number;\r\n  /** Answers that were already down when the clock ran out, if it did. */\r\n  regulationAnsweredQids?: string[];\r\n}\r\n\r\n/**\r\n * Read the stored session for one pool. console.warn(\'[practice-session] migration write failed\', { poolSlug, error });\r\n      }\r\n    }\r\n    return migrated;\r\n  } catch (e) {\r\n    console.warn(\'[practice-session] load failed, loadAllPracticeSessions(\r\n  userId: string,\r\n): Promise<Record<string, PracticeSession>> {\r\n  const slugs = Object.keys(MAIN_CONCLUSION_PRACTICE_POOLS);\r\n  const rows = await });\r\n  return out;\r\n}\r\n\r\n/**\r\n * Write the student\'s current place in a practice set. A caller that must not write over a finished run —\r\n * a homework assignment, whose row is that one sitting — stops calling before\r\n * it gets here (see genericFinalizedRef in Drill.tsx).\r\n */\r\nexport async function savePracticeSessionSnapshot(\r\n  userId: string,\r\n  pool: Pick<PracticePool, \'slug\' | \'completionId\'>,\r\n  snapshot: PracticeSessionSnapshot,\r\n): Promise<void> {\r\n  if (!userId || !pool?.slug) return;\r\n  if (snapshot.questionQueue.length === 0) return;\r\n\r\n  const id = practiceSessionIdFor(userId, pool.slug);\r\n  await withRow(id, async () => {\r\n    const persist = getPersistence(userId);\r\n    const existing = await loadPracticeSession(userId, pool.slug);\r\n\r\n    const record = PracticeSession.parse({\r\n      id,\r\n      user_id: userId,\r\n      pool_slug: pool.slug,\r\n      completion_id: pool.completionId,\r\n      data_version: PRACTICE_SESSION_DATA_VERSION,\r\n      question_queue: snapshot.questionQueue,\r\n      current_index: Math.min(\r\n        Math.max(snapshot.currentIndex, 0),\r\n        Math.max(snapshot.questionQueue.length - 1, 0),\r\n      ),\r\n      attempts: snapshot.attempts,\r\n      status: \'in_progress\',\r\n      // Never hand back time already spent: a snapshot with no clock keeps\r\n      // whatever the row already knew rather than clearing it.\r\n      elapsed_ms: snapshot.elapsedMs ?? existing?.elapsed_ms,\r\n      regulation_answered_qids:\r\n        snapshot.regulationAnsweredQids ?? existing?.regulation_answered_qids,\r\n      started_at: existing?.started_at ?? now(),\r\n      updated_at: now(),\r\n    });\r\n\r\n    await persist.set(TABLES.practice_sessions, id, record);\r\n  });\r\n}\r\n\r\n/**\r\n * Mark the set finished. The row is kept rather than deleted so the index can\r\n * show what was answered and how it went after completion.\r\n */\r\nexport async function completePracticeSession(\r\n  userId: string,\r\n  pool: Pick<PracticePool, \'slug\' | \'completionId\'>,\r\n  snapshot: PracticeSessionSnapshot,\r\n): Promise<void> {\r\n  if (!userId || !pool?.slug) return;\r\n\r\n  const id = practiceSessionIdFor(userId, pool.slug);\r\n  await withRow(id, async () => {\r\n    const persist = getPersistence(userId);\r\n    const existing = await loadPracticeSession(userId, pool.slug);\r\n\r\n    const record = PracticeSession.parse({\r\n      id,\r\n      user_id: userId,\r\n      pool_slug: pool.slug,\r\n      completion_id: pool.completionId,\r\n      data_version: PRACTICE_SESSION_DATA_VERSION,\r\n      question_queue: snapshot.questionQueue.length\r\n        ? snapshot.questionQueue\r\n        : existing?.question_queue ?? 'text': "### 1\n/**\r\n * Persistence factory.\r\n *\r\n * For signed-in users we use SupabaseSyncedPersistence: local-first reads/writes\r\n * with background autosave to the `bootcamp_kv` table so every profile keeps\r\n * all of their bootcamp progress in the cloud and across devices.\r\n * For anonymous callers we fall back to local-only V1Persistence.\r\n */\r\n\r\nimport type { Persistence } from './Persistence';\r\nimport { V1Persistence } from './V1Persistence';\r\nimport { LocalStoragePersistence } from './LocalStoragePersistence';\r\nimport { IndexedDBPersistence } from './IndexedDBPersistence';\r\nimport { SupabaseSyncedPersistence } from './SupabaseSyncedPersistence';\r\n\r\nconst _cache: Map<string, Persistence> = new Map();\r\nlet _testOverride: Persistence | null = null;\r\n\r\n/** Returns the active adapter scoped to the supplied userId. */\r\nexport function getPersistence(userId: string = 'anon'): Persistence {\r\n  if (_testOverride) return _testOverride;\r\n  const existing = _cache.get(userId);\r\n  if (existing) return existing;\r\n  const created: Persistence =\r\n    userId && userId !== 'anon'\r\n      ? new SupabaseSyncedPersistence(userId)\r\n      : new V1Persistence(\r\n          new LocalStoragePersistence(userId),\r\n          new IndexedDBPersistence(userId),\r\n        );\r\n  _cache.set(userId, created);\r\n  return created;\r\n}\r\n\r\n/** Test hook — inject a fake. Passing null clears any override and cache. */\r\nexport function __setPersistenceForTests(p: Persistence | null): void {\r\n  _testOverride = p;\r\n  if (p === null) _cache.clear();\r\n}\r\n\r\n"}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': "### 2\nImport-Clixml: Unexpected end of file has occurred. These are drawn\r\n      // fresh on every launch, so the stored queue IS the run: adopt it whole\r\n      // rather than comparing it to a new shuffle, or the student loses the\r\n      // questions they already answered. An explicit ?initialIndex wins, since\r\n      // that is someone deliberately jumping to a question.\r\n      if (genericResumeKey && user && !initialIndexParam) {\r\n        const stored = await loadDrillResumeSession(\r\n          user.id,\r\n          genericResumeKey,\r\n          genericLegacyResumeKey,\r\n        );\r\n        // A finished assignment\'s row is that sitting\'s record. This mount\r\n        // must never write over it: a reload during blind review re-mounts\r\n        // the runner with a fresh clock and no answers, and the first answer\r\n        // given on it used to flip the finished row back to in_progress.\r\n        if (assignmentId && stored?.status === \'completed\') {\r\n          genericFinalizedRef.current = true;\r\n        }\r\n        if (isResumableDrillSession(stored)) {\r\n          // Come back on the clock they left. A timed section that resumed\r\n          // with a fresh 35:00 was handing back time already spent, which\r\n          // makes the score it produces meaningless.\r\n          if (stored?.elapsed_ms) pendingResumeElapsedMs.current = stored.elapsed_ms;\r\n          if (stored?.regulation_answered_qids) {\r\n            restoredRegulationMark = { answeredQids: stored.regulation_answered_qids };\r\n          }\r\n          // A row stored at or after the buzzer has no clock left to resume\r\n          // on. It opens on the buzzer instead: answers kept, no time handed\r\n          // back, and the student is asked to finish and score the section.\r\n          const budget =\r\n            mode === \'full-section\'\r\n              ? sectionBudgetMs((state.config as FullSectionConfig | undefined)?.timer)\r\n              : undefined;\r\n          if (isSpentDrillSession(stored, budget)) {\r\n            pendingResumeAtBuzzer.current = true;\r\n            if (!restoredRegulationMark) {\r\n              restoredRegulationMark = { answeredQids: stored!.attempts.map((a) => a.qid) };\r\n            }\r\n          }\r\n          questionQueue = stored.question_queue;\r\n          initialIndex = guidedResumeIndex(\r\n            questionQueue,\r\n            stored.current_index,\r\n            stored.attempts.map((a) => a.qid),\r\n          );\r\n          for (const attempt of stored.attempts) {\r\n            restoredAttempts.set(attempt.qid, {\r\n              selectedAnswer: attempt.selected_answer,\r\n              correct: attempt.correct,\r\n              timeMs: attempt.time_ms,\r\n              timestamp: Date.now(),\r\n              confidence: undefined,\r\n              reviewDone: false,\r\n              // The record, not just the answer. A resumed run used to come\r\n              // back flattened: no changes of mind, no marks for review.\r\n              switchCount: attempt.switch_count,\r\n              brMarked: attempt.flagged,\r\n              answerRevealed: attempt.answer_revealed ?? false,\r\n              answerTrail: attempt.answer_trail?.map((e) => ({\r\n                answer: e.answer,\r\n                atMs: e.at_ms,\r\n              })),\r\n            });\r\n          }\r\n        }\r\n      }\r\n\r\n\r\n      setSession({\r\n        mode,\r\n        regulationMark: restoredRegulationMark,\r\n        fullSectionConfig: mode === \'full-section\' ? (state.config as FullSectionConfig) : undefined,\r\n        typeDrillConfig: (mode === \'type-drill\' || mode === \'natural-drill\') ? (state.config as TypeDrillConfig) : undefined,\r\n        questionQueue,\r\n        currentIndex: initialIndex,\r\n        attempts: restoredAttempts,\r\n      });\r\n    };\r\n\r\n    initializeSession();\r\n  }, [state, navigate, classId, classIdLoading, qbLoading, settings.allowRepeats, settings.preferUnseen, settings.recycleAfterDays, initialIndexParam, isMcPractice, mcPracticePool, guidedSessionKey, guidedLegacySessionKey, genericResumeKey, genericLegacyResumeKey, userId]);\r\n\r\n  // Shapes the student\'s current place into a resume snapshot. Shared by the two\r\n  // surfaces that persist one — Intro to LR practice sets and guided drills — and\r\n  // written on every answer and every question move, so leaving mid-drill is\r\n  // never a loss. The persistence layer writes locally at once and debounces the\r\n  // cloud upsert, so this stays cheap enough to run on each session change.\r\n  /**\r\n   * How much of the section clock has been spent, right now.\r\n   *\r\n   * A countdown reports what is left, so the spend is the budget minus that; a\r\n   * stopwatch already reports the spend. Null when there is no clock at all,\r\n   * which is the difference between "no time used" and "no time to use".\r\n   */\r\n  //\r\n  // Read through a ref: the timer context is a new object on every 250ms\r\n  // tick, and a snapshot function that depended on it made the snapshot\r\n  // effect below fire on every tick: an in_progress write four times a\r\n  // second, and a cloud debounce that never settled while the clock ran.\r\n  const timerRef = React.useRef(timer);\r\n  timerRef.current = timer;\r\n  const sectionTimerConfig = session?.fullSectionConfig?.timer;\r\n  const sectionElapsedMs = React.useCallback((): number | undefined => {\r\n    const clock = timerRef.current;\r\n    if (!clock) return undefined;\r\n    if (clock.mode === \'stopwatch\') return Math.max(0, Math.round(clock.ms));\r\n    const total = sectionBudgetMs(sectionTimerConfig);\r\n    if (!total) return undefined;\r\n    return Math.max(0, Math.round(total - clock.ms));\r\n  }, [sectionTimerConfig]);\r\n\r\n  const drillResumeSnapshot = React.useCallback(\r\n    (source: DrillSession | null): PracticeSessionSnapshot | null => {\r\n      if (!source) return null;\r\n      return {\r\n        questionQueue: source.questionQueue,\r\n        currentIndex: source.currentIndex,\r\n        attempts: Array.from(source.attempts.entries())\r\n          .filter(([, attempt]) => !!attempt.selectedAnswer)\r\n          .map(([qid, attempt]) => ({\r\n            qid,\r\n            selected_answer: attempt.selectedAnswer,\r\n            correct: !!attempt.correct,\r\n            time_ms: Number.isFinite(attempt.timeMs) ? Math.max(0, Math.round(attempt.timeMs)) : 0,\r\n            switch_count: attempt.switchCount ?? 0,\r\n            flagged: !!attempt.brMarked,\r\n            answer_revealed: !!attempt.answerRevealed,\r\n            answer_trail: attempt.answerTrail?.map((e) => ({\r\n              answer: e.answer,\r\n              at_ms: Math.max(0, Math.round(e.atMs)),\r\n            })),\r\n          })),\r\n        // Stored as time SPENT, so it reads the same whether the surface\r\n        // counts down or up.\r\n        elapsedMs: sectionElapsedMs(),\r\n        regulationAnsweredQids: source.regulationMark?.answeredQids,\r\n      };\r\n    },\r\n    [sectionElapsedMs],\r\n  );\r\n\r\n  /**\r\n   * The clock keeps running between answers, and a student can sit on one\r\n   * question for five minutes. Snapshots written only on answer changes would\r\n   * hand those five minutes back on resume, so a timed section also saves on a\r\n   * slow heartbeat. Held in a ref because the snapshot function changes on\r\n   * every tick and would otherwise restart the interval each time.\r\n   */\r\n  const snapshotRef = React.useRef(drillResumeSnapshot);\r\n  snapshotRef.current = drillResumeSnapshot;\r\n  React.useEffect(() => {\r\n    if (!genericResumeKey || !user || !session || !hasTimer) return;\r\n    if (genericFinalizedRef.current) return;\r\n    if (session.mode !== \'full-section\') return;\r\n    const id = window.setInterval(() => {\r\n      // Re-checked on every beat: the section can finish between two of them.\r\n      if (genericFinalizedRef.current) return;\r\n      const snapshot = snapshotRef.current(session);\r\n      if (!snapshot || snapshot.attempts.length === 0) return;\r\n      void saveDrillResumeSnapshot(user.id, genericResumeKey, snapshot).catch((err) => {\r\n        console.error(\'[drill:resume:heartbeat] resume write failed\', { err });\r\n      });\r\n    }, 20_000);\r\n    return () => window.clearInterval(id);\r\n  }, [genericResumeKey, user, session, hasTimer]);\r\n\r\n  React.useEffect(() => {\r\n    if (!isMcPractice || !mcPracticePool || !user || !session) return;\r\n    if (mcPracticeFinalizedRef.current) return;\r\n    if (session.questionQueue.length === 0) return;\r\n    const snapshot = drillResumeSnapshot(session);\r\n    if (!snapshot) return;\r\n    void savePracticeSessionSnapshot(user.id, mcPracticePool, snapshot).catch((err) => {\r\n      // Non-fatal: the drill still works, they just lose the resume point.\r\n      console.error(\'[drill:mc-practice:snapshot] resume write failed\', {\r\n        userId: user.id,\r\n        slug: mcPracticePool.slug,\r\n        err,\r\n      });\r\n    });\r\n  }, [session, isMcPractice, mcPracticePool, user, drillResumeSnapshot]);\r\n\r\n  // Same snapshot on every answer and every question move for guided drills, so\r\n  // the student\'s place survives a tab close, a sign-out, or another device —\r\n  // none of which a URL param can carry.\r\n  React.useEffect(() => {\r\n    if (!guidedSessionKey || !user || !session) return;\r\n    if (guidedFinalizedRef.current) return;\r\n    if (session.questionQueue.length === 0) return;\r\n    const snapshot = drillResumeSnapshot(session);\r\n    if (!snapshot) return;\r\n    void saveGuidedDrillSnapshot(user.id, guidedSessionKey, snapshot).catch((err) => {\r\n      // Non-fatal: the drill still works, they just lose the resume point.\r\n      console.error(\'[drill:guided:snapshot] resume write failed\', {\r\n        userId: user.id,\r\n        key: guidedSessionKey,\r\n        err,\r\n      });\r\n    });\r\n  }, [session, guidedSessionKey, user, drillResumeSnapshot]);\r\n\r\n  // And the same again for every other drill surface. Only runs with at least\r\n  // one answered question are written: an untouched shuffle is not progress,\r\n  // and storing it would freeze a stale question order in place.\r\n  React.useEffect(() => {\r\n    if (!genericResumeKey || !user || !session) return;\r\n    if (genericFinalizedRef.current) return;\r\n    if (session.questionQueue.length === 0) return;\r\n    const snapshot = drillResumeSnapshot(session);\r\n    if (!snapshot || snapshot.attempts.length === 0) return;\r\n    void saveDrillResumeSnapshot(user.id, genericResumeKey, snapshot).catch((err) => {\r\n      // Non-fatal: the drill still works, they just lose the resume point.\r\n      console.error(\'[drill:resume:snapshot] resume write failed\', {\r\n        userId: user.id,\r\n        key: genericResumeKey,\r\n        err,\r\n      });\r\n    });\r\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '### 4\n\r\nsrc/features/rc\\api\\rcSupabase.types.ts:48:export const RC_SESSIONS_TABLE = \'rc_drill_sessions\';\r\nsrc/features/rc\\api\\rcSupabase.types.ts:49:export const RC_ATTEMPTS_TABLE = \'rc_attempts\';\r\nsrc/pages\\AcademyMainhub.tsx:181: * Drill hub stage. The UI does not block on it, but callers that\r\n    // must not fabricate a default over real cloud data await ready() first.\r\n    // hydrate() never rejects (it catches internally), so ready() always resolves.\r\n    this.readyPromise = this.hydrate();\r\n\r\n    // Best-effort flush on tab close.\r\n    if (typeof window !== 'undefined') {\r\n      window.addEventListener('beforeunload', () => {\r\n        void this.flush();\r\n      });\r\n      window.addEventListener('visibilitychange', () => {\r\n        if (document.visibilityState === 'hidden') void this.flush();\r\n      });\r\n    }\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  ready(): Promise<void> {\r\n    return this.readyPromise;\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  private key(table: string, id: string): string {\r\n    return `${table}::${id}`;\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  async hydrate(): Promise<void> {\r\n    if (this.hydrated || !this.userId || this.userId === 'anon') return;\r\n    try {\r\n      const { data, error } = await supabase\r\n        .from('bootcamp_kv')\r\n        .select('table_name, record_id, data')\r\n        .eq('user_id', this.userId);\r\n      if (error) {\r\n        console.warn('[SupabaseSyncedPersistence] hydrate failed', error.message);\r\n        return;\r\n      }\r\n      for (const row of data ?? []) {\r\n        try {\r\n          await this.local.set(row.table_name, row.record_id, row.data as object);\r\n        } catch (e) {\r\n          console.warn('[SupabaseSyncedPersistence] hydrate row failed', e);\r\n        }\r\n      }\r\n      this.hydrated = true;\r\n    } catch (e) {\r\n      console.warn('[SupabaseSyncedPersistence] hydrate threw', e);\r\n    }\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  private scheduleFlush(): void {\r\n    if (this.flushTimer) clearTimeout(this.flushTimer);\r\n    this.flushTimer = setTimeout(() => {\r\n      void this.flush();\r\n    }, FLUSH_DEBOUNCE_MS);\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  async flush(): Promise<void> {\r\n    if (!this.userId || this.userId === 'anon') return;\r\n    if (this.pending.size === 0) return;\r\n    const batch = Array.from(this.pending.values());\r\n    this.pending.clear();\r\n    if (this.flushTimer) {\r\n      clearTimeout(this.flushTimer);\r\n      this.flushTimer = null;\r\n    }\r\n\r\n    const upserts = batch.filter((b) => b.data !== null);\r\n    const deletes = batch.filter((b) => b.data === null);\r\n\r\n    try {\r\n      if (upserts.length > 0) {\r\n        const rows = upserts.map((b) => ({\r\n          user_id: this.userId,\r\n          table_name: b.table,\r\n          record_id: b.id,\r\n          data: b.data as never,\r\n        }));\r\n        const { error } = await supabase\r\n          .from('bootcamp_kv')\r\n          .upsert(rows, { onConflict: 'user_id,table_name,record_id' });\r\n        if (error) {\r\n          console.warn('[SupabaseSyncedPersistence] upsert failed', error.message);\r\n          // Re-queue on failure.\r\n          for (const b of upserts) this.pending.set(this.key(b.table, b.id), b);\r\n          this.scheduleFlush();\r\n        }\r\n      }\r\n      for (const d of deletes) {\r\n        const { error } = await supabase\r\n          .from('bootcamp_kv')\r\n          .delete()\r\n          .eq('user_id', this.userId)\r\n          .eq('table_name', d.table)\r\n          .eq('record_id', d.id);\r\n        if (error) {\r\n          // Student deletes are denied by RLS (admin-only data retention).\r\n          // Drop the delete rather than requeueing forever — the cloud row is\r\n          // intentionally preserved.\r\n          console.warn('[SupabaseSyncedPersistence] delete denied/failed (cloud row preserved)', error.message);\r\n        }\r\n      }\r\n    } catch (e) {\r\n      console.warn('[SupabaseSyncedPersistence] flush threw', e);\r\n      for (const b of batch) this.pending.set(this.key(b.table, b.id), b);\r\n      this.scheduleFlush();\r\n    }\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  // ── Persistence API ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\r\n\r\n  get<T>(table: string, id: string): Promise<T | null> {\r\n    return this.local.get<T>(table, id);\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  async set<T>(table: string, id: string, record: T): Promise<void> {\r\n    await this.local.set(table, id, record);\r\n    this.pending.set(this.key(table, id), { table, id, data: record });\r\n    this.scheduleFlush();\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  async remove(table: string, id: string): Promise<void> {\r\n    await
