Self-loading, because it is mounted in two\r\nsrc/features/rc\\lib\\rcHydration.test.ts:5: * These pin the part that decides what a resumed or reviewed session knows\r\nsrc/features/rc\\lib\\rcHydration.test.ts:140:  describe(\'reveal state, which resume used to drop entirely\', () => {\r\nsrc/features/rc\\lib\\rcHydration.test.ts:223:  it(\'points a resumed drill at the first question with no answer\', () => {\r\nsrc/features/rc\\lib\\rcHydration.ts:2: * What a resumed or reviewed session knows about the student\'s own work.\r\nsrc/features/rc\\lib\\rcHydration.ts:106: * Which reveals survive a resume, per mode.\r\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '### 3\n  on public.section_history (practice_test_run_id, practice_test_leg)\r\n  where practice_test_run_id is not null;\r\n\r\ncreate unique index if not exists rc_sessions_pt_leg_once\r\n  on public.rc_drill_sessions (practice_test_run_id, practice_test_leg)\r\n  where practice_test_run_id is not null;\r\n\r\n-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\r\n-- 1d. RC first-attempt integrity\r\n-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\r\n\r\n-- recordAttempt is read-then-insert with no constraint, so racing writes can\r\n-- already have double-counted. The unique index below will not build over those\r\n-- duplicates, so they are resolved first.\r\n--\r\n-- DEMOTE, do not delete. A duplicate is a real attempt the student made; only\r\n-- its is_first_attempt flag is wrong, and the review surfaces legitimately show\r\n-- it as a later attempt. Ranking is by (created_at, id) and never by ctid, which\r\n-- is physical row location rather than chronology and would retain an arbitrary\r\n-- row as "first".\r\nwith ranked as (\r\n  select id,\r\n         row_number() over (\r\n           partition by session_id, question_id\r\n           order by created_at asc, id asc\r\n         ) as rn\r\n    from public.rc_attempts\r\n   where is_first_attempt\r\n)\r\nupdate public.rc_attempts a\r\n   set is_first_attempt = false\r\n  from ranked r\r\n where a.id = r.id\r\n   and r.rn > 1;\r\n\r\ncreate unique index if not exists rc_attempts_first_once\r\n  on public.rc_attempts (session_id, question_id)\r\n  where is_first_attempt;\r\n\r\n-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\r\n-- 1e. Stale-run cleanup (used by change 5, shipped here so one migration\r\n--     carries all the DDL)\r\n-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\r\n\r\n-- exclude_ids exists for change 5. A run whose dirty retry failed still LOOKS\r\n-- stale, because its offline progress never landed to refresh updated_at.\r\n-- Abandoning it server-side would let the terminal-wins reconcile then destroy\r\n-- that progress, so change 5 passes those ids here. The default keeps a\r\n-- no-argument call valid.\r\ncreate or replace function public.abandon_stale_practice_tests(\r\n  exclude_ids text[] default \'{}\'\r\n)\r\nreturns setof text\r\nlanguage sql\r\nsecurity invoker\r\nset search_path = public\r\nas $$\r\n  update public.practice_test_runs\r\n     set status = \'abandoned\',\r\n         revision = revision + 1\r\n   where user_id = auth.uid()\r\n     and status = \'in-progress\'\r\n     and updated_at < now() - interval \'14 days\'\r\n     and not (id = any (coalesce(exclude_ids, \'{}\')))\r\n  returning id;\r\n$$;\r\n'}]

The restored set\r\n            // goes to `firstAttemptEliminations`, for display only. It is display only: the array written with a re-attempt is\r\n        // built from what they do on this visit, not from this.\r\n        setFirstAttemptEliminations(hydrated.eliminations);\r\n      })\r\n      .catch(() => {\r\n        /* resuming without prior answers is still a usable drill */\r\n      });\r\n\r\n    return () => {\r\n      cancelled = true;\r\n    };\r\n    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps\r\n  }, [passages, sessionId, targetQuestionId]);\r\n\r\n  /* ----------------------------------------------------------- tools */\r\n\r\n  const a11y = useRcA11y();\r\n"}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '### 1\nImport-Clixml: Unexpected end of file has occurred. (os error 123)\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '### 2\nimport * as React from \'react\';\r\nimport { useNavigate } from \'react-router-dom\';\r\nimport { deleteSession, getSessionAttempts, listSessions } from \'../api/rcProgress\';\r\nimport { REVEAL_LABELS, type RcDrillSession } from \'../lib/rcTypes\';\r\n\r\n/**\r\n * A student\'s RC drills: resume an unfinished one, open the results of a\r\n * finished one, delete either. Self-loading, because it is mounted in two\r\n * places (the standalone /rc surface and the drill hub\'s RC practice bay) and\r\n * threading sessions through both callers would give the two a way to disagree\r\n * about what has been drilled.\r\n *\r\n * Scores count FIRST attempts only, so this row and the results page can never\r\n * report different numbers for the same session.\r\n */\r\nexport function RcSessionHistory({ tone = \'light\' }: { tone?: \'light\' | \'dark\' }) {\r\n  const navigate = useNavigate();\r\n  const [sessions, setSessions] = React.useState<RcDrillSession[]>([]);\r\n  const [scores, setScores] = React.useState<Map<string, { correct: number; total: number }>>(\r\n    new Map(),\r\n  );\r\n  const [loaded, setLoaded] = React.useState(false);\r\n  const [error, setError] = React.useState(false);\r\n\r\n  const load = React.useCallback(async (signal?: { cancelled: boolean }) => {\r\n    try {\r\n      const list = await listSessions();\r\n      if (signal?.cancelled) return;\r\n      setSessions(list);\r\n      const done = list.filter((x) => x.status === \'completed\');\r\n      const pairs = await Promise.all(\r\n        done.map(async (x) => {\r\n          const rows = (await getSessionAttempts(x.id)).filter((a) => a.isFirstAttempt);\r\n          return [\r\n            x.id,\r\n            { correct: rows.filter((a) => a.isCorrect === true).length, total: rows.length },\r\n          ] as const;\r\n        }),\r\n      );\r\n      if (!signal?.cancelled) setScores(new Map(pairs));\r\n    } catch {\r\n      /* Not "no drills yet". A failed query — outage, RLS, a missing table —\r\n         is a different thing from an empty history, and rendering the empty\r\n         state for it tells a student their work is gone. */\r\n      if (!signal?.cancelled) setError(true);\r\n    } finally {\r\n      if (!signal?.cancelled) setLoaded(true);\r\n    }\r\n  }, []);\r\n\r\n  React.useEffect(() => {\r\n    const signal = { cancelled: false };\r\n    void load(signal);\r\n    return () => {\r\n      signal.cancelled = true;\r\n    };\r\n  }, [load]);\r\n\r\n  const remove = async (id: string) => {\r\n    const ok = window.confirm(\r\n      \'Delete this drill? Its answers and timings are deleted with it, and that cannot be undone.\',\r\n    );\r\n    if (!ok) return;\r\n    try {\r\n      await deleteSession(id);\r\n      setSessions(await listSessions());\r\n    } catch {\r\n      setError(true);\r\n    }\r\n  };\r\n\r\n  if (error) {\r\n    return (\r\n      <p className="rc-empty">\r\n        Your reading drills could not be loaded. Reload the page to try again.\r\n      </p>\r\n    );\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  if (loaded && sessions.length === 0) {\r\n    return <p className="rc-empty">No reading drills yet.</p>;\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  return (\r\n    <div className={tone === \'dark\' ? \'rc-history rc-embedded\' : \'rc-history\'}>\r\n      {sessions.map((s) => (\r\n        <div\r\n          key={s.id}\r\n          className={`rc-session-row${s.excludedFromAnalytics ? \' rc-session-excluded\' : \'\'}`}\r\n        >\r\n          <span>\r\n            {s.passageIds.length} passage{s.passageIds.length === 1 ? \'\' : \'s\'} ·{\' \'}\r\n            {s.config.timing.mode === \'unlimited\' ? \'untimed\' : \'timed\'} ·{\' \'}\r\n            {REVEAL_LABELS[s.config.reveal]}\r\n          </span>\r\n          <span className="rc-status-chip">\r\n            {s.status === \'in_progress\' ? \'in progress\' : s.status}\r\n          </span>\r\n          {scores.has(s.id) && (\r\n            <span>\r\n              {scores.get(s.id)!.correct} / {scores.get(s.id)!.total}\r\n            </span>\r\n          )}\r\n          {s.excludedFromAnalytics && <span className="rc-status-chip">excluded</span>}\r\n          <span style={{ marginLeft: \'auto\', display: \'flex\', gap: 8 }}>\r\n            {s.status === \'in_progress\' ? (\r\n              <button\r\n                type="button"\r\n                className="rc-chip"\r\n                onClick={() => navigate(\'/rc/drill?sessionId=\' + s.id)}\r\n              >\r\n                Resume\r\n              </button>\r\n            ) : (\r\n              <button\r\n                type="button"\r\n                className="rc-chip"\r\n                onClick={() => navigate(\'/rc/results/\' + s.id)}\r\n              >\r\n                Results\r\n              </button>\r\n            )}\r\n            <button type="button" className="rc-chip" onClick={() => remove(s.id)}>\r\n              Delete\r\n            </button>\r\n          </span>\r\n        </div>\r\n      ))}\r\n    </div>\r\n  );\r\n}\r\n\r\nexport default RcSessionHistory;\r\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '### 3\ncreate table if not exists rc_drill_sessions (\r\n  id uuid primary key default gen_random_uuid(),\r\n  user_id uuid not null references auth.users(id) on delete cascade,\r\n  config jsonb not null,\r\n  passage_ids int[] not null,\r\n  status text not null default \'in_progress\',   -- in_progress | completed | abandoned\r\n  excluded_from_analytics boolean not null default false,\r\n  started_at timestamptz not null default now(),\r\n  completed_at timestamptz\r\n);\r\ncreate table if not exists rc_attempts (\r\n  id uuid primary key default gen_random_uuid(),\r\n  session_id uuid not null references rc_drill_sessions(id) on delete cascade,\r\n  user_id uuid not null references auth.users(id) on delete cascade,\r\n  question_id int not null,\r\n  passage_id int not null,\r\n  selected text,                                 -- A-E or null (skipped)\r\n  is_correct boolean,\r\n  time_ms int not null default 0,\r\n  flagged boolean not null default false,\r\n  eliminations text[] not null default \'{}\',\r\n  is_first_attempt boolean not null default true,\r\n  created_at timestamptz not null default now()\r\n);\r\ncreate table if not exists rc_annotations (\r\n  user_id uuid not null references auth.users(id) on delete cascade,\r\n  passage_id int not null,\r\n  highlights jsonb not null default \'[]\',\r\n  updated_at timestamptz not null default now(),\r\n  primary key (user_id, passage_id)\r\n);\r\nalter table rc_drill_sessions enable row level security;\r\nalter table rc_attempts enable row level security;\r\nalter table rc_annotations enable row level security;\r\ncreate policy "own sessions" on rc_drill_sessions for all using (auth.uid() = user_id) with check (auth.uid() = user_id);\r\ncreate policy "own attempts" on rc_attempts for all using (auth.uid() = user_id) with check (auth.uid() = user_id);\r\ncreate policy "own annotations" on rc_annotations for all using (auth.uid() = user_id) with check (auth.uid() = user_id);\r\ncreate index if not exists rc_attempts_user_q_idx on rc_attempts (user_id, question_id);\r\ncreate index if not exists rc_attempts_session_idx on rc_attempts (session_id);\r\n'}]

'countdown' : 'stopwatch'}\n691:          timerOverBudget={overBudget}\n827:          disabled={state.paused}\n872:                  dispatch({ type: 'setPaused', paused: true });\n"}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '### 1\n/**\r\n * Supabase reads and writes for RC progress: sessions, attempts, annotations.\r\n *\r\n * Content is static JSON (see rcBank.ts). Only the student\'s own work lives in\r\n * the database, and RLS scopes every row to them.\r\n */\r\nimport {\r\n  rcDb,\r\n  RC_ANNOTATIONS_TABLE,\r\n  RC_ATTEMPTS_TABLE,\r\n  RC_SESSIONS_TABLE,\r\n  type RcAttemptRow,\r\n  type RcDrillSessionRow,\r\n} from \'./rcSupabase.types\';\r\nimport type {\r\n  RcAttempt,\r\n  RcDrillConfig,\r\n  RcDrillSession,\r\n  RcHighlight,\r\n  RcLetter,\r\n  RcQuestionHistory,\r\n  RcSessionStatus,\r\n} from \'../lib/rcTypes\';\r\n\r\n/** Postgres "relation does not exist" — the migration has not been run. */\r\nconst MISSING_TABLE = \'42P01\';\r\n\r\nexport class RcTablesMissingError extends Error {\r\n  constructor() {\r\n    super(\r\n      \'The RC progress tables are missing. Run supabase/migrations/20260806000000_rc_drilling.sql.\',\r\n    );\r\n    this.name = \'RcTablesMissingError\';\r\n  }\r\n}\r\n\r\nfunction raise(error: { code?: string; message?: string } | null): void {\r\n  if (!error) return;\r\n  if (error.code === MISSING_TABLE) throw new RcTablesMissingError();\r\n  throw new Error(error.message ?? \'Unknown Supabase error\');\r\n}\r\n\r\n/** Confirms the progress tables exist. Call once on player/hub boot. */\r\nexport async function checkRcTables(): Promise<true> {\r\n  const { error } = await rcDb.from(RC_SESSIONS_TABLE).select(\'id\').limit(1);\r\n  raise(error);\r\n  return true;\r\n}\r\n\r\nasync function requireUserId(): Promise<string> {\r\n  const { data } = await rcDb.auth.getUser();\r\n  const id = data?.user?.id;\r\n  if (!id) throw new Error(\'You need to be signed in to drill.\');\r\n  return id;\r\n}\r\n\r\nfunction toSession(row: RcDrillSessionRow): RcDrillSession {\r\n  return {\r\n    id: row.id,\r\n    userId: row.user_id,\r\n    config: row.config as RcDrillConfig,\r\n    passageIds: row.passage_ids ?? [],\r\n    status: row.status as RcSessionStatus,\r\n    excludedFromAnalytics: row.excluded_from_analytics,\r\n    startedAt: row.started_at,\r\n    completedAt: row.completed_at,\r\n  };\r\n}\r\n\r\nfunction toAttempt(row: RcAttemptRow): RcAttempt {\r\n  return {\r\n    id: row.id,\r\n    sessionId: row.session_id,\r\n    userId: row.user_id,\r\n    questionId: row.question_id,\r\n    passageId: row.passage_id,\r\n    selected: (row.selected as RcLetter | null) ?? null,\r\n    isCorrect: row.is_correct,\r\n    timeMs: row.time_ms,\r\n    flagged: row.flagged,\r\n    eliminations: (row.eliminations ?? []) as RcLetter[],\r\n    isFirstAttempt: row.is_first_attempt,\r\n    createdAt: row.created_at,\r\n  };\r\n}\r\n\r\n/* ----------------------------------------------------------------- sessions */\r\n\r\nexport async function createSession(\r\n  config: RcDrillConfig,\r\n  passageIds: number[],\r\n): Promise<RcDrillSession> {\r\n  const userId = await requireUserId();\r\n  const { data, error } = await rcDb\r\n    .from(RC_SESSIONS_TABLE)\r\n    .insert({ user_id: userId, config, passage_ids: passageIds })\r\n    .select()\r\n    .single();\r\n  raise(error);\r\n  return toSession(data as RcDrillSessionRow);\r\n}\r\n\r\nexport async function getSession(id: string): Promise<RcDrillSession | null> {\r\n  const { data, error } = await rcDb.from(RC_SESSIONS_TABLE).select().eq(\'id\', id).maybeSingle();\r\n  raise(error);\r\n  return data ? toSession(data as RcDrillSessionRow) : null;\r\n}\r\n\r\nexport async function listSessions(): Promise<RcDrillSession[]> {\r\n  const { data, error } = await rcDb\r\n    .from(RC_SESSIONS_TABLE)\r\n    .select()\r\n    .order(\'started_at\', { ascending: false });\r\n  raise(error);\r\n  return ((data ?? []) as RcDrillSessionRow[]).map(toSession);\r\n}\r\n\r\nexport async function completeSession(id: string): Promise<void> {\r\n  const { error } = await rcDb\r\n    .from(RC_SESSIONS_TABLE)\r\n    .update({ status: \'completed\', completed_at: new Date().toISOString() })\r\n    .eq(\'id\', id);\r\n  raise(error);\r\n}\r\n\r\nexport async function setSessionExcluded(id: string, excluded: boolean): Promise<void> {\r\n  const { error } = await rcDb\r\n    .from(RC_SESSIONS_TABLE)\r\n    .update({ excluded_from_analytics: excluded })\r\n    .eq(\'id\', id);\r\n  raise(error);\r\n}\r\n\r\nexport async function deleteSession(id: string): Promise<void> {\r\n  const { error } = await rcDb.from(RC_SESSIONS_TABLE).delete().eq(\'id\', id);\r\n  raise(error);\r\n}\r\n\r\n/* ----------------------------------------------------------------- attempts */\r\n\r\n/**\r\n * Writes one attempt row. The first write for a question within a session is the\r\n * first attempt; every later write for that same question, review re-answers\r\n * included, is a new row flagged `is_first_attempt: false`. Existing rows are\r\n * never updated, so the original answer stays intact.\r\n */\r\nexport async function recordAttempt(attempt: RcAttempt): Promise<RcAttempt> {\r\n  const userId = attempt.userId ?? (await requireUserId());\r\n\r\n  const { data: prior, error: priorError } = await rcDb\r\n    .from(RC_ATTEMPTS_TABLE)\r\n    .select(\'id\')\r\n    .eq(\'session_id\', attempt.sessionId)\r\n    .eq(\'question_id\', attempt.questionId)\r\n    .limit(1);\r\n  raise(priorError);\r\n\r\n  const isFirstAttempt = (prior ?? []).length === 0;\r\n\r\n  const { data, error } = await rcDb\r\n    .from(RC_ATTEMPTS_TABLE)\r\n    .insert({\r\n      session_id: attempt.sessionId,\r\n      user_id: userId,\r\n      question_id: attempt.questionId,\r\n      passage_id: attempt.passageId,\r\n      selected: attempt.selected,\r\n      is_correct: attempt.isCorrect,\r\n      time_ms: attempt.timeMs,\r\n      flagged: attempt.flagged,\r\n      eliminations: attempt.eliminations,\r\n      is_first_attempt: isFirstAttempt,\r\n    })\r\n    .select()\r\n    .single();\r\n  raise(error);\r\n  return toAttempt(data as RcAttemptRow);\r\n}\r\n\r\nexport async function getSessionAttempts(sessionId: string): Promise<RcAttempt[]> {\r\n  const { data, error } = await rcDb\r\n    .from(RC_ATTEMPTS_TABLE)\r\n    .select()\r\n    .eq(\'session_id\', sessionId)\r\n    .order(\'created_at\', { ascending: true });\r\n  raise(error);\r\n  return ((data ?? []) as RcAttemptRow[]).map(toAttempt);\r\n}\r\n\r\n/** Sets the flag on every stored attempt for a question in a session. */\r\nexport async function setAttemptFlag(\r\n  sessionId: string,\r\n  questionId: number,\r\n  flagged: boolean,\r\n): Promise<void> {\r\n  const { error } = await rcDb\r\n    .from(RC_ATTEMPTS_TABLE)\r\n    .update({ flagged })\r\n    .eq(\'session_id\', sessionId)\r\n    .eq(\'question_id\', questionId);\r\n  raise(error);\r\n}\r\n\r\n/**\r\n * Per-question aggregate over the student\'s whole history, used by the hub\'s\r\n * status filters and by adaptive selection.\r\n */\r\nexport async function getQuestionHistory(userId?: string): Promise<RcQuestionHistory> {\r\n  const uid = userId ?? (await requireUserId());\r\n  const { data, error } = await rcDb\r\n    .from(RC_ATTEMPTS_TABLE)\r\n    .select(\'question_id, is_correct, created_at\')\r\n    .eq(\'user_id\', uid)\r\n    .order(\'created_at\', { ascending: true });\r\n  raise(error);\r\n\r\n  const history: RcQuestionHistory = new Map();\r\n  for (const row of (data ?? []) as Pick<\r\n    RcAttemptRow,\r\n    \'question_id\' | \'is_correct\' | \'created_at\'\r\n  >[]) {\r\n    const existing = history.get(row.question_id) ?? {\r\n      questionId: row.question_id,\r\n      attempts: 0,\r\n      everIncorrect: false,\r\n      incorrectOnLastTake: false,\r\n      incorrectCount: 0,\r\n      lastSeenAt: null as string | null,\r\n    };\r\n    const wrong = row.is_correct === false;\r\n    existing.attempts += 1;\r\n    if (wrong) {\r\n      existing.everIncorrect = true;\r\n      existing.incorrectCount += 1;\r\n    }\r\n    // rows arrive oldest first, so the last one seen decides the latest take\r\n    existing.incorrectOnLastTake = wrong;\r\n    existing.lastSeenAt = row.created_at;\r\n    history.set(row.question_id, existing);\r\n  }\r\n  return history;\r\n}\r\n\r\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------- annotations */\r\n\r\nexport async function loadAnnotations(\r\n  passageIds: number[],\r\n): Promise<Map<number, RcHighlight[]>> {\r\n  const result = new Map<number, RcHighlight[]>();\r\n  if (passageIds.length === 0) return result;\r\n\r\n  const { data, error } = await rcDb\r\n    .from(RC_ANNOTATIONS_TABLE)\r\n    .select(\'passage_id, highlights\')\r\n    .in(\'passage_id\', passageIds);\r\n  raise(error);\r\n\r\n  for (const row of (data ?? []) as { passage_id: number; highlights: unknown }[]) {\r\n    result.set(row.passage_id, (row.highlights ?? []) as RcHighlight[]);\r\n  }\r\n  return result;\r\n}\r\n\r\nconst saveTimers = new Map<number, ReturnType<typeof setTimeout>>();\r\n\r\n/** Debounced by one second per passage, so dragging out a highlight writes once. */\r\nexport function saveAnnotations(passageId: number, highlights: RcHighlight[]): void {\r\n  const pending = saveTimers.get(passageId);\r\n  if (pending) clearTimeout(pending);\r\n  saveTimers.set(\r\n    passageId,\r\n    setTimeout(() => {\r\n      saveTimers.delete(passageId);\r\n      void saveAnnotationsNow(passageId, highlights);\r\n    }, 1000),\r\n  );\r\n}\r\n\r\nexport async function saveAnnotationsNow(\r\n  passageId: number,\r\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '### TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.practice_test_assignments (\r\n  id          uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),\r\n  student_id  uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES auth.users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,\r\n  pt          integer NOT NULL,\r\n  assigned_by uuid,\r\n  assigned_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),\r\n  unlock_at   timestamptz,\r\n  status      text NOT NULL DEFAULT \'assigned\'\r\n              CHECK (status IN (\'assigned\', \'completed\', \'archived\')),\r\n  UNIQUE (student_id, pt)\r\n);\r\n\r\nCREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS practice_test_assignments_student_idx\r\n  ON public.practice_test_assignments (student_id);\r\n\r\nALTER TABLE public.practice_test_assignments ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;\r\n\r\nDROP POLICY IF EXISTS "Admins manage practice test assignments" ON public.practice_test_assignments;\r\nCREATE POLICY "Admins manage practice test assignments"\r\n  ON public.practice_test_assignments FOR ALL\r\n  USING (public.has_role(auth.uid(), \'admin\'))\r\n  WITH CHECK (public.has_role(auth.uid(), \'admin\'));\r\n\r\nDROP POLICY IF EXISTS "Students read own practice test assignments" ON public.practice_test_assignments;\r\nCREATE POLICY "Students read own practice test assignments"\r\n  ON public.practice_test_assignments FOR SELECT\r\n  USING (\r\n    student_id = auth.uid()\r\n    AND status <> \'archived\'\r\n    AND (unlock_at IS NULL OR unlock_at <= now())\r\n  );\r\n\r\n-- True when this user may start a sitting of this PrepTest: the Practice tests\r\n-- switch, an admin, or a live assignment for exactly this PT.\r\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.can_sit_practice_test(_user uuid, _pt integer)\r\n RETURNS boolean\r\n LANGUAGE sql\r\n STABLE\r\n SECURITY DEFINER\r\n SET search_path TO \'public\'\r\nAS $function$\r\n  SELECT\r\n    public.has_role(_user, \'admin\')\r\n    OR EXISTS (\r\n      SELECT 1 FROM public.profiles\r\n      WHERE class_id = _user::text AND has_practice_access\r\n    )\r\n    OR EXISTS (\r\n      SELECT 1 );\r\n$function$;\r\n\r\n-- The API cannot be used to start a sitting the student was not given.\r\nDROP POLICY IF EXISTS pt_runs_owner_insert ON public.practice_test_runs;\r\nCREATE POLICY pt_runs_owner_insert\r\n  ON public.practice_test_runs FOR INSERT\r\n  WITH CHECK (user_id = auth.uid() AND public.can_sit_practice_test(auth.uid(), pt));\r\n\r\n-- An assigned sitting shows on \'smartboard_recap\', \'activity\',\r\n    \'practice_test\']));\r\n\r\n-- Explicit, though the schema\'s default privileges already grant these. RLS\r\n-- above decides which rows each role actually sees.\r\nGRANT SELECT ON public.practice_test_assignments TO authenticated;\r\nGRANT ALL ON public.practice_test_assignments TO service_role;\r\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '### 1\n/**\r\n * Handing one leg of a practice test to the runner that already exists, and\r\n * reading its result back.\r\n *\r\n * The sitting owns sequence, clock and scoring. It does NOT own answering —\r\n * that stays in `/drill` (LR) and `/rc` (RC), so a question inside a practice\r\n * test behaves identically to the same question outside one. The cost of that\r\n * choice is this file: the runners report to their own tables, and the sitting\r\n * has to go and find the row afterwards.\r\n *\r\n * A leg is matched by "this user, this section, finished after the moment the\r\n * leg began". That is why `startedAt` is stamped before navigating and never\r\n * after.\r\n */\r\nimport { supabase } from \'@/integrations/supabase/client\';\r\nimport type { FullSectionConfig, TimerMode } from \'@/types/drill\';\r\nimport {\r\n  practiceTestRunSectionSeconds,\r\n  type PracticeTestLeg,\r\n  type PracticeTestRun,\r\n} from \'@/lib/practiceTest\';\r\nimport { RC_STANDARD_SECONDS_PER_PASSAGE } from \'@/features/rc/lib/rcTypes\';\r\n\r\nconst PRACTICE_TEST_PATH_PREFIX = \'/testing/test/\';\r\n\r\nexport function practiceTestPath(runId: string): string {\r\n  return `${PRACTICE_TEST_PATH_PREFIX}${runId}`;\r\n}\r\n\r\n/**\r\n * Whether a return path points back into a sitting.\r\n *\r\n * ROUTING ONLY: it decides where a runner navigates and whether score UI is\r\n * suppressed. It must never gate a clock or an integrity rule, because the path\r\n * it reads is student-editable. Anything load-bearing reads persisted run and\r\n * leg attribution instead.\r\n *\r\n * The prefix lives here, next to the function that writes it, so the two cannot\r\n * drift apart.\r\n */\r\nexport function isSittingReturn(path: string | null | undefined): boolean {\r\n  return !!path?.startsWith(PRACTICE_TEST_PATH_PREFIX);\r\n}\r\n\r\n/**\r\n * The clock a leg runs on.\r\n *\r\n * This used to map the pace id straight onto an absolute TimerMode: \'standard\'\r\n * became \'35\' no matter what the student\'s standing timing mode said, so a\r\n * student carrying extra time who chose "Standard" was handed a 35-minute\r\n * section for every leg of a two-hour sitting. The seconds are resolved against\r\n * their own clock when the run is created, and read back here, so the pace is\r\n * relative for everyone. \'custom\' carries the exact figure, including the\r\n * fractional 52.5 and 78.75 minute cases, which the named modes cannot express.\r\n */\r\nfunction timerForRun(run: PracticeTestRun): { mode: TimerMode; customMinutes?: number } {\r\n  const seconds = practiceTestRunSectionSeconds(run);\r\n  if (seconds === null) return { mode: \'unlimited\' };\r\n  return { mode: \'custom\', customMinutes: seconds / 60 };\r\n}\r\n\r\n/**\r\n * Which run and which leg a runner was launched for.\r\n *\r\n * The runner needs this to key its closeout bookkeeping, and it cannot be\r\n * recovered from `returnTo`: that string is a routing hint, editable and\r\n * removable, and clock integrity must never depend on it. The leg INDEX is\r\n * passed rather than searched for, because `legs` can hold two legs of the same\r\n * section and matching by section number would pick the wrong one.\r\n */\r\nexport interface PtLegIdentity {\r\n  runId: string;\r\n  legIndex: number;\r\n}\r\n\r\nexport interface LrLegLaunch {\r\n  pathname: \'/drill\';\r\n  state: {\r\n    mode: \'full-section\';\r\n    config: FullSectionConfig;\r\n    returnTo: string;\r\n    practicingLabel: string;\r\n    ptRun: PtLegIdentity;\r\n  };\r\n}\r\n\r\nexport function lrLegLaunch(\r\n  run: PracticeTestRun,\r\n  leg: PracticeTestLeg,\r\n  legIndex: number,\r\n): LrLegLaunch {\r\n  return {\r\n    pathname: \'/drill\',\r\n    state: {\r\n      mode: \'full-section\',\r\n      config: {\r\n        pt: run.pt,\r\n        section: leg.section,\r\n        timer: {\r\n          ...timerForRun(run),\r\n          isPaused: false,\r\n          elapsedMs: 0,\r\n          startedAt: Date.now(),\r\n        },\r\n      },\r\n      returnTo: practiceTestPath(run.id),\r\n      practicingLabel: `Practice Test ${run.pt}`,\r\n      ptRun: { runId: run.id, legIndex },\r\n    },\r\n  };\r\n}\r\n\r\n/**\r\n * The RC leg\'s link. `reveal=end_of_set` because a section is sat under test\r\n * conditions — nothing is revealed until the sitting is over, and inside a\r\n * practice test that is doubly true.\r\n */\r\nexport function rcLegHref(\r\n  run: PracticeTestRun,\r\n  leg: PracticeTestLeg,\r\n  legIndex: number,\r\n): string {\r\n  const secondsPerPassage = (() => {\r\n    // Same reading as the LR leg: the run\'s own resolved clock, so an\r\n    // accommodated student\'s RC passages get their share of the longer section\r\n    // rather than a share of 35 minutes.\r\n    const sectionSeconds = practiceTestRunSectionSeconds(run);\r\n    if (sectionSeconds === null) return null;\r\n    const passages = leg.passageIds?.length || 4;\r\n    return Math.round(sectionSeconds / passages);\r\n  })();\r\n\r\n  const params = new URLSearchParams({\r\n    passages: (leg.passageIds ?? []).join(\',\'),\r\n    reveal: \'end_of_set\',\r\n    timing: secondsPerPassage === null ? \'untimed\' : \'standard\',\r\n    start: \'1\',\r\n    returnTo: practiceTestPath(run.id),\r\n    ptRun: run.id,\r\n    ptLeg: String(legIndex),\r\n  });\r\n  if (secondsPerPassage !== null && secondsPerPassage !== RC_STANDARD_SECONDS_PER_PASSAGE) {\r\n    params.set(\'secondsPerPassage\', String(secondsPerPassage));\r\n  }\r\n  return `/rc?${params.toString()}`;\r\n}\r\n\r\nexport interface LegResult {\r\n  correct: number;\r\n  answered: number;\r\n  timeMs: number;\r\n}\r\n\r\n/**\r\n * Read one finished LR leg out of `section_history`.\r\n *\r\n * Returns null when no matching row exists yet, which is the normal answer for\r\n * a leg the student walked away from rather than completed. The caller decides\r\n * what to do about that; this never invents a zero, because a zero here reads\r\n * as "answered everything wrong" on the score report.\r\n */\r\nexport async function readLrLegResult(\r\n  userId: string,\r\n  pt: number,\r\n  section: number,\r\n  startedAtIso: string,\r\n): Promise<LegResult | null> {\r\n  const { data, error } = await supabase\r\n    .from(\'section_history\')\r\n    .select(\'initial_score, initial_total, total_questions, total_time_ms, time_taken_ms, completed_at\')\r\n    .eq(\'user_id\', userId)\r\n    .eq(\'pt\', pt)\r\n    .eq(\'section\', section)\r\n    .gte(\'completed_at\', startedAtIso)\r\n    .order(\'completed_at\', { ascending: false })\r\n    .limit(1);\r\n\r\n  if (error || !data || data.length === 0) return null;\r\n  const row = data[0];\r\n  return {\r\n    correct: row.initial_score ?? 0,\r\n    answered: row.initial_total ?? row.total_questions ?? 0,\r\n    timeMs: row.total_time_ms ?? row.time_taken_ms ?? 0,\r\n  };\r\n}\r\n\r\n/**\r\n * Read one finished RC leg out of `rc_drill_sessions` + `rc_attempts`.\r\n *\r\n * Matched on the passage set rather than a section number: RC sessions record\r\n * which passages were sat, not which section they came from.\r\n */\r\nexport async function readRcLegResult(\r\n  userId: string,\r\n  passageIds: number[],\r\n  startedAtIso: string,\r\n): Promise<LegResult | null> {\r\n  const { data: sessions, error } = await supabase\r\n    .from(\'rc_drill_sessions\')\r\n    .select(\'id, passage_ids, completed_at\')\r\n    .eq(\'user_id\', userId)\r\n    .eq(\'status\', \'completed\')\r\n    .gte(\'completed_at\', startedAtIso)\r\n    .order(\'completed_at\', { ascending: false })\r\n    .limit(5);\r\n\r\n  if (error || !sessions || sessions.length === 0) return null;\r\n\r\n  const wanted = [...passageIds].sort((a, b) => a - b).join(\',\');\r\n  const session = sessions.find(\r\n    (s) => [...(s.passage_ids ?? [])].sort((a, b) => a - b).join(\',\') === wanted,\r\n  );\r\n  if (!session) return null;\r\n\r\n  const { data: attempts } = await supabase\r\n    .from(\'rc_attempts\')\r\n    .select(\'is_correct, time_ms\')\r\n    .eq(\'session_id\', session.id)\r\n    .eq(\'is_first_attempt\', true);\r\n\r\n  if (!attempts) return null;\r\n  return {\r\n    correct: attempts.filter((a) => a.is_correct).length,\r\n    answered: attempts.length,\r\n    timeMs: attempts.reduce((n, a) => n + (a.time_ms ?? 0), 0),\r\n  };\r\n}\r\n\r\nexport async function readLegResult(\r\n  userId: string,\r\n  run: PracticeTestRun,\r\n  leg: PracticeTestLeg,\r\n): Promise<LegResult | null> {\r\n  if (!leg.startedAt) return null;\r\n  return leg.kind === \'lr\'\r\n    ? readLrLegResult(userId, run.pt, leg.section, leg.startedAt)\r\n    : readRcLegResult(userId, leg.passageIds ?? [], leg.startedAt);\r\n}\r\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': "### 2\n  const item = getCurrentItem(state);\r\n  const type = React.useMemo(\r\n    () => rcTypeStyles(a11y.fontScale, a11y.extraLeading),\r\n    [a11y.fontScale, a11y.extraLeading],\r\n  );\r\n\r\n  /* ---------------------------------------------------------- clocks */\r\n\r\n  const [elapsed, setElapsed] = React.useState(0);\r\n  const questionStart = React.useRef<number>(Date.now());\r\n\r\n  React.useEffect(() => {\r\n    if (state.paused) return;\r\n    const id = window.setInterval(() => setElapsed((e) => e + 1), 1000);\r\n    return () => window.clearInterval(id);\r\n  }, [state.paused]);\r\n\r\n  React.useEffect(() => {\r\n    questionStart.current = Date.now();\r\n  }, [state.currentIndex]);\r\n\r\n  const budget = totalBudgetSeconds(config, passages?.length ?? 0);\r\n  const clockSeconds = budget == null ? elapsed : budget - elapsed;\r\n  const overBudget = budget != null && clockSeconds < 0;\r\n\r\n  /* --------------------------------------------------------- writing */\r\n\r\n  const writeAttempt = React.useCallback(\r\n    async (target: RcSessionItem, selected: RcLetter | null): Promise<boolean> => {\r\n      if (!sessionId) return false;\r\n      // A question already written once in this session is not written again.\r\n      // Resume used to lose the reveal state, which left Confirm enabled on a\r\n      // question that was already answered and inserted a second attempt row\r\n      // for it. The reveal state is restored now; this is the belt.\r\n      if (state.recorded.has(target.question.questionId)) return true;\r\n      const timeMs = Math.max(0, Date.now() - questionStart.current);\r\n      try {\r\n        await recordAttempt({\r\n          sessionId,\r\n          questionId: target.question.questionId,\r\n          passageId: target.passage.passageId,\r\n          selected,\r\n          isCorrect: selected ? selected === target.question.correctAnswer : null,\r\n          timeMs,\r\n          flagged: state.flagged.has(target.question.questionId),\r\n          eliminations: state.eliminations.get(target.question.questionId) ?? [],\r\n          isFirstAttempt: true,\r\n        });\r\n        dispatch({ type: 'markRecorded', questionIds: [target.question.questionId] });\r\n        return true;\r\n      } catch {\r\n        /*\r\n         * A failed write used to return quietly and the caller carried on:\r\n         * Confirm revealed, the passage moved into review, and submit walked to\r\n         * the results screen — all of it telling the student work was recorded\r\n         * when none of it was. Losing their place is still the wrong cure, so\r\n         * the drill stays where it is and says what happened instead.\r\n         */\r\n        setSaveError('That answer did not save.');\r\n        return false;\r\n      }\r\n    },\r\n    [sessionId, state.flagged, state.eliminations, state.recorded],\r\n  );\r\n\r\n  const recordPassage = React.useCallback(\r\n    async (passageId: number) => {\r\n      const items = itemsForPassage(state, passageId).filter(\r\n        (i) => !state.recorded.has(i.question.questionId),\r\n      );\r\n      let allSaved = true;\r\n      for (const target of items) {\r\n        const saved = await writeAttempt(\r\n          target,\r\n          state.answers.get(target.question.questionId) ?? null,\r\n        );\r\n        if (!saved) allSaved = false;\r\n      }\r\n      // Feedback is the reward for a recorded answer. Opening it over a write\r\n      // that failed would show the student a verdict on work nothing holds.\r\n      if (allSaved) dispatch({ type: 'reviewPassage', passageId });\r\n      return allSaved;\r\n    },\r\n    [state, writeAttempt],\r\n  );\r\n\r\n  /* ------------------------------------------------------ navigation */\r\n\r\n  const goTo = React.useCallback(\r\n    (index: number) => {\r\n      const leaving = getCurrentItem(state);\r\n      // end_of_set writes silently as the student moves on.\r\n      if (\r\n        leaving &&\r\n        config.reveal === 'end_of_set' &&\r\n        !state.recorded.has(leaving.question.questionId) &&\r\n        state.answers.has(leaving.question.questionId)\r\n      ) {\r\n        void writeAttempt(leaving, state.answers.get(leaving.question.questionId) ?? null);\r\n      }\r\n      dispatch({ type: 'goto', index });\r\n    },\r\n    [state, config.reveal, writeAttempt],\r\n  );\r\n\r\n  const handleSubmit = React.useCallback(async () => {\r\n    for (const target of state.items) {\r\n      if (!state.recorded.has(target.question.questionId)) {\r\n        const saved = await writeAttempt(\r\n          target,\r\n          state.answers.get(target.question.questionId) ?? null,\r\n        );\r\n        // Stop at the first failure. Completing the session and walking to a\r\n        // results screen built on answers that were never stored is the worst\r\n        // version of this: the student sees a score for work that is gone.\r\n        if (!saved) return;\r\n      }\r\n    }\r\n    dispatch({ type: 'submit' });\r\n    if (sessionId) {\r\n      try {\r\n        await completeSession(sessionId);\r\n      } catch {\r\n        setSaveError('The drill did not finish saving.');\r\n        return;\r\n      }\r\n      // A leg of a practice test goes straight back to the invigilator. The\r\n      // session is already completed in the database by this point, so the\r\n      // sitting can score it; showing the results screen first would reveal a\r\n      // section score with sections still to sit. RC is frequently section 1\r\n      // or 2, so this is the common case, not an edge one.\r\n      if (isSittingReturn(returnTo)) {\r\n        navigate(returnTo);\r\n        return;\r\n      }\r\n      // Carried, not dropped: anything else with a returnTo has somewhere to be\r\n      // after the results screen, and only this query string knows where.\r\n      navigate(`/rc/results/${sessionId}${returnTo ? `?returnTo=${encodeURIComponent(returnTo)}` : ''}`);\r\n    }\r\n  }, [state, writeAttempt, sessionId, navigate, returnTo]);\r\n\r\n  const toggleFlag = React.useCallback(\r\n    (questionId: number) => {\r\n      dispatch({ type: 'toggleFlag', questionId });\r\n      if (sessionId) {\r\n        void setAttemptFlag(sessionId, questionId, !state.flagged.has(questionId));\r\n      }\r\n    },\r\n    [sessionId, state.flagged],\r\n  );\r\n"}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': "### 3\nimport type { Persistence, PersistenceTx } from './Persistence';\r\n\r\nconst PREFIX = 'student';\r\n\r\n/** Per architecture-plan.md §3.2 — KV via `student:{userId}:{table}:{id}` keys. */\r\nexport class LocalStoragePersistence implements Persistence {\r\n  constructor(private userId: string = 'anon') {}\r\n\r\n  ready(): Promise<void> { return Promise.resolve(); }\r\n\r\n  private k(table: string, id: string): string {\r\n    return `${PREFIX}:${this.userId}:${table}:${id}`;\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  private prefix(table: string): string {\r\n    return `${PREFIX}:${this.userId}:${table}:`;\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  async get<T>(table: string, id: string): Promise<T | null> {\r\n    const raw = localStorage.getItem(this.k(table, id));\r\n    if (raw == null) return null;\r\n    try {\r\n      return JSON.parse(raw) as T;\r\n    } catch {\r\n      return null;\r\n    }\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  async set<T>(table: string, id: string, record: T): Promise<void> {\r\n    try {\r\n      localStorage.setItem(this.k(table, id), JSON.stringify(record));\r\n    } catch (err) {\r\n      console.warn('[LocalStoragePersistence] quota exceeded or write failed', err);\r\n      throw err;\r\n    }\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  async remove(table: string, id: string): Promise<void> {\r\n    localStorage.removeItem(this.k(table, id));\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  async list<T>(table: string, filter?: Partial<T>): Promise<T[]> {\r\n    const out: T[] = [];\r\n    const p = this.prefix(table);\r\n    for (let i = 0; i < localStorage.length; i++) {\r\n      const key = localStorage.key(i);\r\n      if (!key || !key.startsWith(p)) continue;\r\n      const raw = localStorage.getItem(key);\r\n      if (!raw) continue;\r\n      try {\r\n        const rec = JSON.parse(raw) as T;\r\n        if (!filter || matchesFilter(rec, filter)) out.push(rec);\r\n      } catch {\r\n        // skip malformed\r\n      }\r\n    }\r\n    return out;\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  // Blob storage is delegated to IndexedDBPersistence by V1Persistence; calling\r\n  // these on the LocalStorage adapter directly is a programmer error.\r\n  async putBlob(_key: string, _blob: Blob): Promise<void> {\r\n    throw new Error('LocalStoragePersistence does not support blobs — use IndexedDBPersistence');\r\n  }\r\n  async getBlob(_key: string): Promise<Blob | null> {\r\n    throw new Error('LocalStoragePersistence does not support blobs — use IndexedDBPersistence');\r\n  }\r\n  async removeBlob(_key: string): Promise<void> {\r\n    throw new Error('LocalStoragePersistence does not support blobs — use IndexedDBPersistence');\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  async transaction<T>(fn: (tx: PersistenceTx) => Promise<T>): Promise<T> {\r\n    // localStorage has no native transactions; we approximate by collecting writes\r\n    // and applying atomically. For v1 the simple semantics are sufficient.\r\n    const tx: PersistenceTx = {\r\n      get: <U>(t: string, i: string) => this.get<U>(t, i),\r\n      set: <U>(t: string, i: string, r: U) => this.set<U>(t, i, r),\r\n    };\r\n    return fn(tx);\r\n  }\r\n}\r\n\r\nfunction matchesFilter<T>(record: T, filter: Partial<T>): boolean {\r\n  return Object.entries(filter).every(\r\n    ([k, v]) => (record as Record<string, unknown>)[k] === v,\r\n  );\r\n}\r\n"}]

Long-term population studies complicated the ranking by showing that health outcomes track what replaces a discarded fat: swapping it for refined starches yields no benefit, while swapping it for certain plant oils does. The chemistry was not wrong, and some substitutions really are improvements. The revision is in the unit of advice. A section that has already been carried past its buzzer must\r\n  // not be asked again on every tick.\r\n  React.useEffect(() => {\r\n    if (!timer || !session) return;\r\n    if (session.mode !== 'full-section') return;\r\n    if (timer.mode !== 'countdown' || timer.ms > 0) return;\r\n    if (overtimeState !== 'none' || session.regulationMark) return;\r\n\r\n    const answeredQids = Array.from(session.attempts.entries())\r\n      .filter(([, a]) => !!a.selectedAnswer)\r\n      .map(([qid]) => qid);\r\n    setSession({ ...session, regulationMark: { answeredQids } });\r\n    timer.pause();\r\n    setOvertimeState('prompt');\r\n  }, [timer, session, overtimeState]);\r\n\r\n  // Metadata for the drawer's Details tab, drawn from the active question.\r\n"}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '### 2\nsupabase/migrations/20260829120000_practice_test_runs.sql:87:  break_started_at timestamptz,\r\nsrc/integrations/supabase/types.ts:3336:          break_started_at: string | null\r\nsrc/integrations/supabase/types.ts:3356:          break_started_at?: string | null\r\nsrc/integrations/supabase/types.ts:3376:          break_started_at?: string | null\r\n'}]
